*Sara Ghazanfari,Siddharth Garg,Prashanth Krishnamurthy,Farshad Khorrami*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SYNCR是一个基于模拟器（Habitat/Kubric/CLEVRER）的跨视频推理评测与训练框架，用共享任务生成器同时产出可靠标签与定向监督，含8类跨视频任务、4000评测题与15960训练题，揭示22个多模态大模型的持续失败点并证明合成监督可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨视频推理需要对齐事件、匹配身份、比较运动并整合部分观察，但评测这类能力既缺乏可靠标签也缺乏定向监督数据；作者希望用同一套模拟器生成机制同时解决"评测可信度"与"监督信号"两个问题。

Method: 以Habitat、Kubric、CLEVRER为底座构建SYNCR框架，由环境状态直接推导答案，用共享任务生成器在互不重叠的视频上生成8类跨视频推理任务的评测集（4000题）与训练集（15960题）；通过视觉消融与人工评估检验答案对给定证据的依赖性与可恢复性；对22个MLLM做零样本评测，并对Qwen3-VL-8B做监督微调，再迁移到Assembly101、Panoptic等真实视频及已有时间推理基准。

Result: 22个多模态大模型在物理比较与场景整合上持续失败，且增大模型规模不能稳定解决；监督微调将Qwen3-VL-8B平均准确率从32.6%提升至61.6%，增益可泛化到未见过的任务配置与视频来源；向真实视频的迁移在时间排序上最稳定，在Assembly101与Panoptic构造集上跨两个模型家族、两种规模、三个检查点提升9.0–20.5个百分点，并在已有时间推理基准上有额外增益。

Conclusion: SYNCR提供了一个可控环境，用于诊断跨视频推理失败、检验其可学习性，并识别合成监督在何处可迁移到真实数据；其核心价值是评测+训练一体的模拟器接地框架，而非具体机器人系统。

Abstract: Reasoning across videos requires aligning events, matching identities, comparing motion, and integrating partial observations. Evaluating these capabilities and testing how to improve them requires both reliable labels and targeted supervision. We introduce SYNCR, a simulator-grounded framework that connects these two needs through shared task generators. Built on Habitat, Kubric, and CLEVRER, SYNCR derives answers from environment state and provides 4,000 evaluation questions and 15,960 training questions over disjoint videos, spanning eight cross-video reasoning tasks. Visual ablations and human evaluation assess dependence on the supplied evidence and answer recoverability. Evaluation of 22 multimodal large language models reveals persistent difficulties in physical comparison and scene integration that increasing model size does not consistently resolve. Supervised fine-tuning raises Qwen3-VL-8B's average SYNCR accuracy from 32.6% to 61.6%, with gains extending to task configurations and video sources absent from training for those tasks. Transfer to real footage is most consistent for temporal ordering: accuracy improves by 9.0-20.5 percentage points on constructed Assembly101 and Panoptic ordering sets across three checkpoints spanning two model families and two model sizes, with additional gains on existing temporal reasoning benchmarks. These results establish SYNCR as a controlled setting for diagnosing cross-video reasoning failures, testing their learnability, and identifying where synthetic supervision transfers.

</details>


### [203] [EpiCon: Collective Agent Learning through Co-Evolving Multimodal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37923)
*Ziyun Zeng,Hang Hua,Shaden Alshammari,Rogerio Feris,William T. Freeman,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EpiCon：一个不更新宿主模型参数的共享多模态记忆框架，通过记忆控制器与树式自组织器（各2B）实现agent间的经验复用与集体学习，在11个基准上平均提升1.7–4.9分并将记忆操作耗时降低67%–74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent可从历史执行中学习，但不同agent之间难以复用并累积彼此经验。现有做法通常需微调宿主模型参数或依赖单体记忆，缺乏跨agent、跨任务的共享经验库，尤其在多模态任务中难以同时复用文本指导与视觉证据。

Method: 提出EpiCon共享多模态记忆框架，冻结宿主模型参数。用两个独立训练的2B模型：记忆控制器在多次尝试中联合精炼文本指导与视觉证据，并选择性地纳入视觉记忆；树自组织器将经验教训按层级固化，并为新问题检索经验与规则。问题级记忆演化与持久经验库相链接，形成经验银行（experience bank）。

Result: 在覆盖4个多模态任务域的11个基准上、两种harness与多种backbone下评测：冻结的经验库即使用单次求解尝试也能提升其他系统；第二种harness使原系统macro-average提升2.6分；在四种宿主配置下较No Memory提升macro-average 1.7–4.9分，同时相对backbone规模的记忆模型减少67%–74%的记忆操作时间。

Conclusion: EpiCon表明无需更新宿主参数即可通过共享、分层、多模态的持久经验库实现agent集体学习，兼顾效果提升与推理效率，具备跨任务域与跨backbone的通用性。

Abstract: Agents can learn from past executions, but enabling different agents to reuse and build on one another's experience remains challenging. We introduce EpiCon, a shared multimodal memory framework for agent collective learning without updating host model parameters. EpiCon links question-level memory evolution to a persistent experience bank through two independently trained 2B models: a memory controller and a tree self-organizer. The controller jointly refines textual guidance and visual evidence across attempts and selectively includes visual memory. The self-organizer consolidates lessons hierarchically and retrieves experience and rules for new problems. We evaluate EpiCon on eleven benchmarks spanning four multimodal task domains, using two harnesses and multiple backbones. A frozen bank improves other systems even with a single solving attempt. A second harness raises the original system's macro-average score by 2.6 points across eleven benchmarks. Across four host configurations, EpiCon improves macro-average scores by 1.7 to 4.9 points over No Memory and reduces memory-operation time by 67\% to 74\% relative to backbone-sized memory models.

</details>


### [204] [Rollout-Marginal Distillation for Long-Horizon Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37925)
*Chenjian Gao,Zhihao Hu,Jianqi Ma,Jun Zhang,Weidong Zhang,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Rollout-Marginal Distillation（RMD），将自回归视频扩散的 rollout 训练拆分为"逐块独立评分（chunk-level marginal）+ 视频级 DMD 恢复时序一致性"，缓解长 rollout 中误差累积与质量退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散支持低延迟流式生成，但长 rollout 中预测误差不断累积；现有视频级分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）对整个 rollout 联合评分，块的质量评估受过去与未来上下文牵连，为维持时序一致性可能去匹配上下文中的伪影，导致视觉质量信号被污染。

Method: RMD 两阶段：1）保留生成历史作为 AR 预测条件，但对每个 chunk 单独与 chunk 级 teacher 做分布匹配评分，使质量修正不受不完美时序上下文干扰；2）随后施加视频级 DMD，以弥补独立逐块评分缺失的时序上下文，恢复时序连贯性。

Result: 实验表明 RMD 在远超训练时域（training horizon）的长 rollout 上仍保持高视觉质量，并优于视频级 DMD 基线；已公开代码与视频结果。

Conclusion: 将 rollout 级联合评分解耦为"逐块边缘分布匹配 + 视频级一致性蒸馏"，是一种简单有效的 AR 视频扩散长时推理质量保持方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion enables low-latency, streamable video generation, but prediction errors often accumulate over long rollouts. Training the generator on its own rollouts exposes it to these imperfect histories. However, existing video-level distribution matching distillation (DMD) scores the whole rollout jointly. Because a chunk is evaluated together with its past and future, its correction can favor matching artifacts in the surrounding context merely to preserve temporal consistency. To provide a clearer visual-quality signal, we introduce Rollout-Marginal Distillation (RMD). RMD retains the generated history for AR prediction but scores each chunk independently against a chunk teacher, ensuring its quality correction is not compromised by an imperfect temporal context. To compensate for the lack of temporal context in independent chunk scoring, RMD subsequently applies video-level DMD to restore temporal coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RMD maintains high visual quality far beyond its training horizon and outperforms video-level DMD baselines. Code and video results are available at this https URL

</details>


### [205] [Look Closer: Patch-wise Supervision for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37937)
*Zhida Zhang,Tao Wu,Siyu Liu,Jie Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究patch级监督（显式裁剪+各自损失，推理时才平均）对AI生成图像检测的影响，跨4个CNN/Transformer骨干在GenImage上均优于整图监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于单patch检测：小RGB区域即保留合成证据，因此探索显式的局部输入与patch级监督这一简单组合是否能提升检测器的泛化性，且无需手工残差滤波或学习式图像级融合模块。

Method: 共享骨干网络对显式裁剪出的patch分别分类，每个patch单独计算损失，仅在推理阶段对patch概率求平均；不引入手工残差滤波或学习的图像级融合模块。实验涵盖单patch选择、多生成器集合、四种CNN与Transformer骨干，并进行监督粒度、源分辨率、裁剪尺寸、推理覆盖度对比以及后处理与困难图像评估。

Result: 在GenImage上，patch级监督变体在全部四个骨干上平均准确率均优于整图监督的对应版本；通过监督粒度、分辨率、裁剪大小与推理覆盖度的对比刻画了方法特性，后处理与困难图像测试揭示了其局限。历史实验包含基于评估的模型选择，因此未作为统一筛选的排行榜比较呈现。

Conclusion: 显式局部输入与patch级监督是一种简单且有效的组合，可作为研究可泛化AI生成图像检测的实用基线；但存在局限，且需注意模型选择带来的评估偏差。

Abstract: How much of an image does a detector need to see? Small RGB regions can retain useful evidence of image synthesis even when they reveal little of the full scene. Motivated by single-patch detection, we study patch-wise supervision: a shared backbone classifies explicit crops, each crop receives its own loss, and patch probabilities are averaged only at inference. The procedure requires neither handcrafted residual filtering nor a learned image-level fusion module. Experiments span single-patch selection, multiple generator collections, and four CNN and Transformer backbones. On GenImage, the reported patch-wise variants improve average accuracy over their whole-image counterparts across all four backbones. Comparisons of supervision granularity, source resolution, crop size, and inference coverage further characterize the approach, while post-processing tests and difficult-image evaluation reveal its limitations. The historical experiments include evaluation-based model selection, so their scores are not presented as a uniformly selected leaderboard comparison. Overall, the study identifies explicit local input and patch-level supervision as a simple, useful combination for investigating generalizable AI-generated image detection.

</details>


### [206] [Does Local Video Understanding Transfer Across Encounters? The EgoGears Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37938)
*Yuedong Tan,Lei Qi,Yu Liu,Di Wen,Ruiping Liu,Xiaoye Wang,Yufan Chen,Junwei Zheng,Chengzhi Wu,Chen Zhang,Zhihang Chen,Haiwen Sun,Zongwei Wu,Radu Timofte,Danda Pani Paudel,Kunyu Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoGears：一个含 567 道单视频与 1,487 道多视频问题的第一视角基准，用于诊断具身系统在跨视角/运动/光照变化下知识迁移能力，发现多视频推理平均下降 22.5 个百分点，瓶颈为观测-证据绑定与有序路线状态跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视频聚合准确率把"局部感知失败"与"观测身份保持、对应建立、证据组合失败"混为一谈，无法判断局部视频理解是否真正可迁移。作者希望把这一"从单视频到多视频的迁移"过程单独拆解、可诊断。

Method: 构建 EgoGears 基准：基于 126 段人类采集的第一视角录像、覆盖 39 条户外路线；含 567 道单视频题与 1,487 道多视频题，其中 531 题需跨独立录像对齐。通过同一物理环境在不同移动速度与光照条件下的重复遍历实现可比对照。单视频题考察局部视觉/空间/运动证据，多视频题考察证据是否仍绑定于正确观测并可组合成一致的路线关系。主榜评测 6 个模型族共 29 种单视频与 31 种多视频 MLLM 配置，并在固定答案格式与评分方式下做受控比较。

Result: 在 20 个可双向比较的配置中，所有模型在多视频题目上均更差，平均下降 22.5 个百分点；该差距在固定答案格式与评分后依旧存在，且不能简单归因于视频数量增加或录像边界。核心瓶颈是观测-证据绑定与有序路线状态跟踪。

Conclusion: 跨视频性能下降并非单纯"视频更多更难"，而是具身多视频理解中观测-证据绑定与路线状态有序跟踪两项能力的缺失；EgoGears 提供了诊断这一迁移过程的互补性评测工具，代码与基准已公开。

Abstract: Embodied systems must make knowledge acquired during one encounter usable in another despite changes in viewpoint, motion, and illumination. Yet aggregate cross-video accuracy conflates failures of local perception with failures to preserve observation identity, establish correspondence, and compose evidence, obscuring whether local video understanding actually transfers. We introduce EgoGears, a complementary single- and multi-video benchmark designed to diagnose this transition. It contains 567 single-video and 1,487 multi-video questions derived from 126 human-collected egocentric recordings covering 39 outdoor routes. Repeated traversals across movement speeds and lighting conditions ground comparisons in shared physical environments; 531 questions require alignment across independent recordings. Single-video questions measure the local visual, spatial, and motion evidence available to a model, while multi-video questions test whether evidence remains bound to the correct observation and can be composed into consistent route relationships. We report 29 single-video and 31 multi-video MLLM configurations across six model families in the main leaderboard. Among the 20 configurations evaluated comparably on both splits, every model performs worse on multi-video questions, with a mean decrease of 22.5 percentage points, and the gap persists when answer format and scoring are held fixed. The gap is not explained simply by additional videos or recording boundaries. The central bottlenecks are observation--evidence binding and ordered route-state tracking. The code and benchmark are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [207] [SoL-Refiner: Speed-of-Light One-Step Refinement for High-Resolution Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37969)
*Haozhe Liu,Tian Ye,Shuchen Xue,Yitong Li,Junsong Chen,Haopeng Li,Jincheng Yu,Duomin Wang,Ruihua Zhang,Lei Zhu,Song Han,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SoL-Refiner，一个单步视频精修器，将低分辨率视频输出一次去噪提升至 4K，通过高分辨率持续训练+RL 后训练+一步蒸馏三阶段实现，并构建 Refiner-Bench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率视频生成成本随时空 token 数快速增长；常见做法是先低分辨率生成再精修，但多步精修会引入第二个采样瓶颈，需要一种高效的一步式精修方案。

Method: 三阶段训练配方：高分辨率持续训练、强化学习（RL）后训练、最终一步蒸馏；并构建 Refiner-Bench 视频精修基准，采用共享输入协议在约 2K 分辨率下对比各精修器。

Result: 在 2K 下，单步 SoL-Refiner 在 VBench 与 UniPercept 均值上优于所有外部精修器；在 3840×2176 下两项指标均超过三步 LTX-2.3 Refiner；完整加速组合在 2K 延迟设置下相对同基线获得 8.91× 的精修延迟加速。

Conclusion: 一步蒸馏式精修可有效替代多步精修，在 2K 与 4K 分辨率上兼顾质量与延迟，为高分辨率视频生成提供高效路径。

Abstract: High-resolution video generation is expensive, as its cost grows rapidly with the number of spatiotemporal tokens. A practical alternative first generates a lower-resolution video and then applies a refiner, but conventional multi-step refinement introduces a second sampling bottleneck. We present SoL-Refiner, a one-step video refiner that transforms low-resolution model outputs into 4K videos with a single denoising step. Our three-stage recipe combines high-resolution continual training, reinforcement learning (RL) post-training, and a final one-step distillation. We introduce Refiner-Bench, a video refinement benchmark constructed from the outputs of different video generators, and use a shared-input protocol to compare refiners at approximately 2K output resolution. At 2K, the one-step SoL-Refiner outperforms all external refiners on the VBench and UniPercept averages, while at $3840\!\times\!2176$ it improves both metrics over the three-step LTX-2.3 Refiner. With the complete acceleration stack, SoL-Refiner achieves an $8.91\times$ speedup in refinement latency over the same baseline in our 2K latency setting.

</details>


### [208] [ORMA: Optimization-based Monocular 4D Reconstruction of Articulated Animals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37986)
*Xuyi Hu,Francesco Palandra,Shangzhe Wu,Daniel Cremers,Riccardo Marin,Silvia Zuffi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ORMA：无需训练的单目四足动物4D重建框架，解耦关节运动与形状，用SMAL+配准结合生成式3D先验重建实例几何，并用DINO对应与时间一致性优化，配套PAW4D合成基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频恢复动物4D表示受限于四足形态多样性与缺乏动物4D监督数据。现有学习方法逐帧处理、依赖合成或模型拟合的3D监督，继承强参数化先验的约束，对分布外物种泛化差：只能得到看似合理的姿态但几何不准，因为底层形状模型无法忠实表达观测实例。

Method: 提出ORMA，训练无关的重建框架，将关节（articulation）与形状（shape）解耦：以预测姿态作为优化参考，同时利用生成式3D先验进行精确形状重建。给定参考图像，重建动物几何并配准到参数化模型SMAL+，得到适配观测实例的关节化形状；再将逐帧关节姿态估计与全局一致的相机位姿结合，在共享世界坐标系中恢复动物运动，并利用自监督DINO对应与时间一致性进一步细化重建。同时提出PAW4D合成多物种基准，提供真值3D几何与相机运动。

Result: 在PAW4D、PFERD及挑战性野外视频上的实验表明，ORMA提升了重建精度，并在多种四足物种上恢复全局一致的动物运动。

Conclusion: 通过解耦关节与形状并引入生成式3D先验，ORMA在无需训练与4D监督的条件下实现对分布外四足物种的几何准确、运动全局一致的4D重建，超越了依赖参数化先验的既有方法。

Abstract: Recovering articulated 4D representations of animals from monocular videos remains challenging due to the large diversity of quadruped morphologies and lack of animal 4D supervision data. Existing learning-based reconstruction methods operate on individual images and rely on synthetic or model-fitted 3D supervision, which inherits the constraints of strong parametric priors and limits generalization to out-of-distribution species. When applied to out-of-distribution animals, they often recover a plausible pose while producing inaccurate geometry because the underlying shape model cannot faithfully represent the observed instance. We present ORMA, a training-free reconstruction framework that decouples articulation from shape, using the predicted pose as reference for optimization while leveraging generative 3D priors for accurate shape reconstruction. Given a reference image, we reconstruct the animal geometry and register it to the parametric model SMAL+, yielding an articulated shape adapted to the observed instance. We then combine per-frame articulated pose estimates with globally consistent camera poses to recover animal motion in a shared world coordinate frame, and further refine the reconstruction using self-supervised DINO correspondences and temporal consistency. To enable quantitative evaluation, we introduce PAW4D, a synthetic multi-species benchmark with ground-truth 3D geometry and camera motion. Experiments on PAW4D, PFERD, and challenging in-the-wild videos demonstrate that ORMA improves reconstruction accuracy while recovering globally consistend animal motion across diverse quadruped species.

</details>


### [209] [HybridCUA: Learning to Orchestrate GUI and CLI for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38008)
*Tongbo Chen,Junbo Niu,Zhengxi Lu,Niu Lian,Fei Tang,Yuchen Yan,Yike Hong,Yong Du,Yizhou Liu,Bofan Chen,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出混合 GUI 与 CLI 交互的计算机使用智能体（CUA）范式 HybridCUA，构建含 5K 混合轨迹与 3K 可验证 RLVR 任务的数据集，采用 SFT+CLI 感知强化学习两阶段训练，在 OSWorld 上达到 53.6%，较基座模型提升 14.8 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CUA 要么只依赖 GUI 交互，效率低且错误率高；要么为 GUI 附加特定应用的 API/工具，需大量工程投入且难以跨应用扩展。作者主张下一代 CUA 应结合 GUI 的通用性与 shell 命令的高效性。核心难题在于当前模型不知道何时、如何调用 CLI。

Method: 数据侧：设计数据构建流水线，生成三类轨迹（纯 GUI、纯 CLI、GUI-CLI 交错），形成 HybridCUA-8K（5K 混合轨迹 + 3K 经校验的 RLVR 任务）。训练侧：两阶段框架——先在构建轨迹上做监督微调，再进行强化学习，RL 采用 CLI 感知奖励，鼓励智能体有选择且可靠地使用 CLI。

Result: HybridCUA-9B 在 OSWorld 上取得 53.6% 准确率，相比基座模型提升 14.8 个百分点；在 WindowsAgentArena 上提升 4.0 个百分点，验证了跨平台泛化性。

Conclusion: 混合 GUI 与 CLI 的范式对计算机使用智能体有效且具备跨平台通用性，两阶段训练与 CLI 感知奖励是释放该范式潜力的关键。

Abstract: Computer use agents (CUAs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in completing digital tasks. However, existing CUAs either rely solely on graphical user interface (GUI) interactions, which are often inefficient and error prone, or augment GUI interactions with application specific APIs or tools, which require substantial engineering effort and are difficult to scale across applications. We argue that the next generation of CUAs should combine GUI interactions with the command line interface (CLI), leveraging the generality of the GUI and the efficiency of shell commands. A critical challenge, however, is that current models do not know when or how to use the CLI during task execution. To address this challenge, we develop a data construction pipeline that produces three types of trajectories: GUI only, CLI only, and interleaved GUI and CLI trajectories. This pipeline results in HybridCUA-8K, containing 5K hybrid trajectories and 3K verified RLVR tasks. Building on these data, we propose a training framework with two stages: supervised fine tuning on the constructed trajectories, followed by reinforcement learning with our CLI aware rewards that encourages agents to use the CLI selectively and reliably. Experiments show that HybridCUA-9B achieves 53.6% accuracy on OSWorld, improving over the base model by 14.8 percentage points, and improves performance on WindowsAgentArena by 4.0 percentage points. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and cross platform generalizability of the hybrid GUI and CLI paradigm for computer use agents.

</details>


### [210] [From Unity Simulation to Diffusion-Based Augmentation: Quantifying Dataset Balance for Robust Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38010)
*Mohamed Benkedadra,Aissa Saoudi,Maxime Gloesener,Sidi Ahmed Mahmoudi,Matei Mancas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统对比Unity仿真渲染与可控扩散生成（CIA）两类数据生成范式，在真实数据稀缺条件下的目标检测迁移效果，并探索真实/仿真/生成数据的最优混合比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在施工安全监测等关键领域，真实数据采集成本高、危险且受伦理约束，需借助合成或生成数据缓解数据稀缺，但仿真与生成式增强各自的迁移能力及最优混合策略尚缺乏统一受控的对比研究。

Method: 提出统一实验框架，在相同模型与训练设置下可控地混合真实、Unity仿真渲染和可控扩散生成（CIA）三类数据源；使用Precision、Recall、mAP及自定义Δ指标定量评估。

Result: 单一合成源均非最优：仅Unity训练mAP@0.5相对真实数据下降约50%，仅CIA下降约16.5%；混合配置显著提升，90%真实+10%Unity取得最佳mAP@0.5为62.68%（较基线+7.64%），90%真实+10%CIA精度最高达74.45%。

Conclusion: 有限比例的合成数据纳入可提升泛化能力，过度替换会引发域漂移；仿真与生成式数据具有互补性，混合策略优于单一来源。

Abstract: Modern computer vision models achieve high accuracy when trained on large-scale annotated datasets. In critical domains such as construction safety monitoring, data collection is costly, hazardous, and ethically constrained. This paper presents a systematic study comparing two complementary data generation paradigms, (1) Unity Simulation-based rendering and (2) Controllable Diffusion-based generation (CIA), for object detection under real data-scarce conditions. A unified experimental framework enables controlled dataset mixing across real, simulated, and generative sources, while maintaining identical model and training settings. Quantitative evaluation using Precision, Recall, mAP, and custom $\Delta$-metrics, reveals that neither simulation nor generative augmentation alone achieves optimal transferability. Unity-only training yields an mAP@0.5 drop of $-50\%$ relative to real data, while CIA-only training shows a milder $-16.5\%$ degradation. Hybrid compositions significantly improve performance, with the 90\% real + 10\% Unity configuration achieving the best overall mAP@0.5 of $62.68\%$ ($+7.64\%$ over baseline), and the 90\% real + 10\% CIA configuration maximizing precision at $74.45\%$. Results demonstrate that limited synthetic inclusion enhances generalization, while excessive substitution induces domain drift.

</details>


### [211] [Beyond Lip Sync: Reference-Grounded Oral Refinement for Audio-Driven Portrait Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38019)
*Bangxun Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RGOR：以同一人enrollment视频为参考、绕开VAE的HD嘴部patch作为条件，并用配对判别器对比参考嘴部，实现保留个人唇齿细节的音频驱动口型同步，并产出评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有lip-sync系统虽同步音频且保持人脸可识别，但生成的是"平均嘴"：唇形与纹理、牙齿排列、露出程度均非该人物本人。根源在于训练与评测都不要求"本人的嘴"——感知损失接受任何合理嘴部，人脸身份主要由嘴周皮肤承载，且inpainting类系统推理代码用未掩码的目标帧作参考从而掩盖了这一问题。

Method: RGOR（Reference-Grounded Oral Refinement）：每帧生成以同一人独立enrollment录像中的帧、以及绕开VAE的HD嘴部patch为条件；训练生成器时引入paired judge，将渲染嘴部与本人参考对比，并学习拒斥"他人的真实嘴部"。

Result: 建立了评估协议，并在留出身份上对比开源与商用lip-sync系统；RGOR在多数指标上取得最好或次好结果，在保持同步与面部其余部分完好的同时保住了本人唇部与牙齿细节。

Conclusion: 个性化（reference-grounded）建模与配对判别监督是解决"平均嘴"问题的有效路径，同时暴露出既有评测因参考帧泄漏而低估该缺陷。

Abstract: We present RGOR (Reference-Grounded Oral Refinement), an audio-driven lip-sync framework that renders the mouth of the specific person being dubbed rather than a generic one. Existing lip-sync systems follow the audio closely and keep the face recognizable, yet the mouth they render is an average mouth: the shape and texture of the lips, the arrangement of the teeth, and how much of them shows as the mouth opens are not that person's. The problem persists because nothing in current training or evaluation asks for the person's own mouth: perceptual losses accept any plausible mouth, face identity is carried mostly by the skin around it, and the released inference code of inpainting systems uses the unmasked target frame as the reference, which hides the gap. To address this, RGOR conditions every generated frame on frames from separate enrollment recordings of the same person and on HD patches of the mouth that bypass the VAE, and trains the generator against a paired judge that compares each rendered mouth with the person's reference and learns to reject a realistic mouth of someone else. We further build an evaluation protocol and use it to compare open-source and commercial lip-sync systems on held-out identities. Experiments show that RGOR achieves the best or second-best result on most metrics, and preserves the person's own lip and dental detail while keeping synchronization and the rest of the face intact.

</details>


### [212] [EVO-WAM: Evolving World Action Models through Video-Action Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38057)
*Shiyang Zhou,Xionghao Wu,Wenbo Li,Shenghe Zheng,Jiyao Zhang,Songsong Yu,Yijun Yang,Jianhui Liu,Haoze Sun,Senqiao Yang,Li Jiang,Jingyong Su,Haoyang Huang,Zhuotao Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EVO-WAM 通过自生成视频-动作轨迹的自迭代训练，将世界动作模型适配到未见任务，无需额外专家演示或真实环境执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略在新任务上泛化需要额外专家演示，成本高。世界动作模型（WAM）联合预测未来视频与动作，可提供监督来源，但生成视频可能未完成任务，或视频看似成功却动作不一致导致执行失败。

Method: 1）在 WAM 训练中加入状态预测与锚定多帧上下文，支持无需外部执行反馈的完整自回归 rollout；2）用视觉语言模型筛选"完成任务"的前缀，并用逆动力学模型验证视频-动作一致性，从而识别可靠训练经验；3）在验证过的前缀上迭代训练 WAM，并用更新后的模型生成新 rollout。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 的 7 个未见任务上，Cosmos3 平均成功率 26.9%→68.0%（约 2.5 倍），DreamZero 28.5%→46.4%（约 1.6 倍）。真实世界 3 个未见长时程复合任务上，Cosmos3 平均成功率 20.0%→76.7%，提升 56.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 自生成轨迹中的可信经验筛选（任务完成性+视频-动作一致性）结合迭代自训练，可使 WAM 在无额外演示、无外部环境执行的情况下显著提升未见任务成功率，并在真机上验证有效性。

Abstract: Improving robot policies on new tasks without collecting additional expert demonstrations remains a central challenge in robot learning. World action models (WAMs) use broad video priors to jointly predict future videos and actions, offering a potential source of supervision for adapting to new tasks. However, generated videos may fail to depict task completion, and even visually successful videos may be paired with inconsistent actions that lead to execution failure. We propose EVO-WAM, a framework that adapts WAMs to unseen tasks by learning from their own generated video-action trajectories, without executing candidate actions in an external environment. First, we augment WAM training with state prediction and anchored multi-frame context to enable complete autoregressive rollouts without external execution feedback. Second, we identify reliable training experience by selecting task-completing prefixes with a vision-language model and verifying their video-action consistency with an inverse dynamics model. Third, we iteratively train the WAM on verified prefixes and generate new rollouts with the updated model. On seven unseen RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, EVO-WAM increases average success rates from 26.9% to 68.0% for Cosmos3 and from 28.5% to 46.4% for DreamZero, reaching approximately $2.5\times$ and $1.6\times$ their initial success rates. On three unseen long-horizon composite tasks in the real world, it improves Cosmos3's average success rate from 20.0% to 76.7%, a gain of 56.7 percentage points. Project Page: this https URL .

</details>


### [213] [RS-OPSD: Reliable Privileged On-Policy-Self-Distillation for Ultra-High-Resolution Remote Sensing VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38072)
*Chengjie Jiang,Yunqi Zhou,Jiafeng Yan,Sihang Zhao,Chun Yuan,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RS-OPSD 提出特权在线策略自蒸馏框架，将"放大细看"的视觉特权内化到模型中，无需推理时视觉搜索或工具调用，在超高分辨率遥感VQA上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高分辨率遥感图像中目标视觉证据极小，现有方法依赖推理期的token剪枝、视觉搜索或工具增强，推理成本高且流程复杂。作者希望探究能否把"局部放大"这一视觉特权在训练阶段内化进模型，从而在推理时零额外开销地利用细粒度证据。

Method: 构建 GeoEvidence-6K（6,750条VQA样本、7类任务、含证据区域标注），并用人类反馈引导的技能精炼（HF-SR）实现可扩展标注；提出 RS-OPSD 特权在线策略自蒸馏框架，含两项关键设计：Context-Preserving Visual Privilege（CPVP）缓解紧裁剪导致上下文丢失，Correctness-Aligned Distillation（CAD）缓解不完美教师带来的冲突信号。

Result: 在 XLRS-Bench、MME-RealWorld-RS、LRS-VQA 三个基准上取得SOTA，平均超出同规模前SOTA约4.0个百分点；2B 变体 RS-OPD-Lite 超过多数8B规模模型并取得最快实测推理速度；代码、数据集与权重均已开源。

Conclusion: 将推理期的视觉放大特权通过特权在线策略自蒸馏内化为模型能力是可行且有效的，可在无额外搜索/工具调用下提升超高分辨率遥感VQA性能，且小模型变体具备强性价比。

Abstract: Ultra-high-resolution (UHR) remote sensing visual question answering (VQA) requires models to resolve small visual evidence within extremely large images. Existing approaches typically rely on token pruning, visual search, or tool-augmented reasoning at inference time. We instead investigate whether the benefit of zoom-in visual privilege can be internalized into the model. We introduce RS-OPSD, a reliable privileged on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) framework for UHR remote sensing VQA. To provide high-quality privileged information with explicit question-relevant evidence, we construct GeoEvidence-6K, containing 6,750 VQA samples across seven task categories with evidence-region annotations, and develop Human Feedback-Guided Skill Refinement (HF-SR) for scalable annotation. To address context loss from tight crops and conflicting signals from imperfect teachers, RS-OPSD introduces Context-Preserving Visual Privilege (CPVP) and Correctness-Aligned Distillation (CAD). Without any additional visual search or tool calls at inference time, RS-OPSD achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on XLRS-Bench, MME-RealWorld-RS, and LRS-VQA, outperforming pervious SOTA models of comparable scale by an average of 4.0 percentage points. Moreover, our 2B variant, RS-OPD-Lite, surpasses most 8B-scale models while achieving the fastest measured inference speed. Our Code, GeoEvidence-6K, and the model weights for RS-OPSD and RS-OPD-Lite are publicly available.

</details>


### [214] [MUGEN: Interactive Panoramic World Exploration via Camera Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38077)
*Jiaming Tan,Zhen Li,Shuwei Shi,Minggui Teng,Siqi Yang,Yuwei Wu,Bo Zheng,Chuanhao Li,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出大规模全景视频数据集 MUGEN（1300+ 小时、4K、含语义与几何标注）与相机可控的全景视频生成模型 Wan360，通过周期性经度 RoPE、ERP 感知填充、随机 roll yaw 与全景 Plücker 嵌入提升 ERP 视频的几何一致性与相机可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式全景视频生成需要同时满足视觉连贯与用户指定相机轨迹，但存在数据与模型的双重缺口：现有全景数据集短、标注弱、缺少相机轨迹；现有相机可控生成模型面向透视视频，无法直接支持球面全景几何。

Method: 构建 MUGEN 大规模真实全景视频数据集（1300+ 小时，≥4K，含语义与几何标注）；在其上训练 Wan360 相机可控交互式全景生成模型。针对 ERP 表示的经度接缝循环性与极点畸变，设计三个无参 ERP 感知组件：周期性经度 RoPE（接缝一致位置编码）、ERP 感知填充（减少边界伪影）、随机 roll yaw（一致性学习）；相机控制采用以 ERP 射线而非透视针孔射线表示运动的全景 Plücker 嵌入。

Result: MUGEN 可作为全景世界探索的数据基础，Wan360 能实现高质量、时间连贯、相机可控的 360° 视频生成。

Conclusion: 面向全景几何的数据与模型协同设计有效弥补了现有缺口，为交互式 360° 世界探索与可控全景视频生成提供了数据与模型基础。

Abstract: Interactive panoramic video generation aims to synthesize immersive 360\textdegree{} videos that remain visually coherent while following user-specified camera trajectories during exploration. However, progress is limited by a coupled data-and-model gap: existing panoramic video datasets are often short, weakly annotated, or lack camera trajectories, while existing camera-controlled video generation models are designed for perspective videos and do not directly support panoramic geometry. In this paper, we introduce MUGEN and Wan360 to address these limitations. MUGEN is a large-scale real-world panoramic video dataset tailored to interactive 360-degree world exploration, comprising over 1,300 hours of at least 4K panoramic videos with rich semantic and geometric annotations. Built on MUGEN, we further present Wan360, a camera-controllable interactive panoramic video generation model. Panoramic videos are commonly represented by EquiRectangular Projection (ERP), which unfolds a spherical 360-degree view into a rectangular frame with cyclic longitude seams and pole distortions. To this end, Wan360 introduces three parameter-free ERP-aware components: periodic longitude RoPE for seam-consistent positional encoding, ERP-aware padding for reducing boundary artifacts, and random roll yaw for consistent learning. For camera control, Wan360 uses a panoramic Plücker embedding that represents camera motion with ERP rays rather than perspective pinhole rays. Experiments show that MUGEN serves as a data foundation for panoramic world exploration, and that Wan360 enables high-quality, temporally coherent, camera-controllable 360-degree video generation.

</details>


### [215] [OmniTaskonomy: When Does Visual Generation Improve Visual Understanding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38079)
*Jiaxin Ge,Yiming Qin,Ji Xie,Haozhe Jiang,Xiaochuang Han,Junyi Zhang,Andrew Dai,Yinfei Yang,Jitendra Malik,Ranjay Krishna,Sewon Min,Haiwen Feng,Le Xue,Baifeng Shi,Trevor Darrell,XuDong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉生成监督（I2I）可提升视觉理解（I2T），增益随生成数据量增加；作者提出 OmniTaskonomy 分类体系（19 个 I2I 任务、25 个 I2T 能力）刻画选择性迁移，并用梯度对齐解释迁移强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型被认为蕴含几何、空间关系与物体性等感知能力，但生成监督对视觉理解是否有益、在何种条件下有益尚不清楚。作者希望厘清"何时"与"如何"通过生成监督提升理解能力。

Method: 构建控制变量式的 I2I 生成与 I2T 理解配对任务（同一底层问题的不同输出模态）；在受控配方下比较不同 I2I 数据量的下游迁移。进一步提出 OmniTaskonomy 统一分类体系，覆盖 19 个 I2I 生成任务与 25 个 I2T 理解能力，系统测量跨任务迁移矩阵。最后计算生成与理解任务之间的梯度方向对齐度，与迁移增益做关联分析。

Result: 在合适训练配方下 I2I 训练提升下游 I2T 性能，且生成数据越多增益越大；迁移图呈现任务依赖的选择性收益，既有符合直觉的对应（深度预测→度量 3D 推理、物体指向→计数、拼图重建→2D 排序），也有意外联系（2.5D 分割→类别识别、Z-depth 预测→定位）；梯度对齐越强，下游迁移增益越大。

Conclusion: 视觉生成是视觉理解的丰富监督来源，其收益可通过恰当的训练课程与任务选择来系统性释放，梯度对齐可作为预测迁移收益的指标。

Abstract: Training a model to generate visual content can encourage it to learn rich perceptual capabilities related to geometry, spatial relationships, and objectness; yet, its benefits for visual understanding remain unclear. We ask: when and how does visual generation supervision improve visual understanding? We study controlled pairs of image-to-image (I2I) generation and image-to-text (I2T) understanding tasks that express the same underlying problem in different output modalities. We find that under the correct recipe, I2I training improves downstream I2T performance, with larger gains as the amount of I2I training data increases. We next ask which generation tasks benefit which understanding capabilities. To study transfer beyond paired tasks, we introduce OmniTaskonomy, a unified taxonomy spanning 19 I2I generation tasks and 25 I2T understanding capabilities. The resulting transfer map reveals selective, task-dependent benefits. Some follow intuitive correspondences, e.g., depth prediction improving metric 3D reasoning, object pointing improving counting, and jigsaw reconstruction improving 2D ordering. Interestingly, we also uncover surprising connections: 2.5D segmentation improving category recognition and Z-depth prediction improving localization. To probe these patterns, we analyze gradient alignment between generation and understanding tasks and find that stronger alignment is associated with larger downstream transfer gains. Together, our results highlight visual generation as a rich source of supervision for visual understanding and provide a roadmap for unlocking its benefits through the right training curriculum and task selection. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [216] [VISTA: Internalizing Collective Visual Experience via On-Policy Distillation for Active Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38086)
*Zheng Jiang,Houde Qian,Yiming Chen,Ling Li,Chaoyang Li,Yueqi Li,Yuxuan Liu,Lifeng Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA通过在线策略蒸馏将同输入多条rollout中的视觉发现转化为共享监督（CVED），并用异质性感知策略改进（HAPI）强化成功轨迹、对失败轨迹做经验引导蒸馏，从而让主动多模态智能体在保持自身交互历史与视觉工具的前提下，内化集体视觉经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动多模态智能体依赖视觉工具获取任务相关证据，但强化学习虽为每个输入采样多条交互轨迹，基于结果的目标函数通常只用这些轨迹估计标量优势，导致各轨迹中互补的视觉发现被大量浪费。因此需要一种机制把同一输入下不同轨迹的视觉经验相互共享，提升学习效率与性能。

Method: 提出VISTA框架：1）集体视觉经验蒸馏（CVED），将同输入rollout中的观测按其交互上下文组织，并与个体决策对齐；2）异质性感知策略改进（HAPI），强化成功轨迹，同时为不成功尝试提供经验引导蒸馏；3）引入经验条件教师，对学生采样响应的前缀进行评估，使一条轨迹的发现能指导另一条轨迹的学习，且不替换学生原始历史、不生成新目标轨迹。训练后智能体保留视觉工具并基于自身交互历史行动。

Result: 在同类规模的主动多模态智能体中取得最强平均性能，并在细粒度感知与通用推理任务上持续优于同骨干训练基线，验证了集体经验对主动多模态学习的价值。

Conclusion: 将同一输入下多轨迹的集体视觉经验通过在线策略蒸馏内化为共享监督，是一种有效提升主动多模态智能体视觉工具使用与推理能力的学习范式。

Abstract: Active multimodal agents use visual tools to acquire task-relevant evidence while reasoning. Although reinforcement learning samples multiple interaction trajectories per input, outcome-based objectives primarily use the group to estimate scalar advantages, leaving complementary visual discoveries underused. We introduce VISTA, which internalizes collective visual experience through on-policy distillation by turning observations from same-input rollouts into shared supervision. Collective visual experience distillation (CVED) organizes these observations with their interaction context and aligns them with individual decisions, while heterogeneity-aware policy improvement (HAPI) reinforces successful trajectories and provides experience-guided distillation for unsuccessful attempts. An experience-conditioned teacher evaluates the student's sampled response prefixes, allowing discoveries from one trajectory to guide learning in another without replacing the student's original history or generating new target trajectories. The trained agent retains its visual tools and acts using its own interaction history. VISTA achieves the strongest average performance among the evaluated active multimodal agents of comparable size and consistently outperforms same-backbone training baselines across fine-grained perception and general reasoning tasks, demonstrating the value of collective experience for active multimodal learning.

</details>


### [217] [Self-Aligned Forcing: Streaming Video Diffusion with Differentiable Noisy History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38114)
*Weiqiang Wang,Zhuokun Chen,Yusheng Dai,Boying Li,Yi Zhang,Hossein Rahmani,Qiuhong Ke,Jianfei Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Self-Aligned Forcing (SAF) 训练方案，通过让历史 K/V 的噪声水平与当前去噪块对齐并保持历史可微，实现自回归视频扩散长时生成，训练快 1.8 倍、4 卡 49.1 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散支持交互式流式生成，但长序列 rollout 存在误差累积；self-rollout 训练虽缓解 exposure bias，有限步 rollout 仍无法解决长程漂移。作者观察到历史 K/V 的噪声水平在视觉质量与运动之间权衡，且恢复历史梯度能让因果训练更接近双向训练。

Method: SAF 将每个块的历史与当前去噪块的噪声水平对齐：历史为前一区块在同一去噪阶段产生的 K/V，使同一阶段所有块可在因果掩码下单次前向去噪；保持噪声历史可微，使未来损失能优化其编码方式，从而省去无梯度 rollout 与逐块 timestep-zero 重缓存。

Result: 训练速度比先前方法快至 1.8 倍且显存更低；推理时在现有方法中单卡吞吐最高，多卡流水线每阶段保留一个 history bank，4 卡达 49.1 FPS；实验显示长时程生成更优，视觉质量与运动平衡更好。

Conclusion: SAF 是一种高效的自回归视频扩散训练与推理方案，在长时程生成质量与吞吐上均有优势。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion enables interactive streaming generation, but suffers from error accumulation over long rollouts. Self-rollout training reduces exposure bias, yet finite rollouts leave long-range drift unresolved. We observe that the noise level of the history key-value (K/V) representations trades visual quality against motion, and that restoring gradients through the history aligns causal training far more closely with bidirectional training. Motivated by these observations, we introduce Self-Aligned Forcing (SAF), a training scheme that aligns the history of each block with the noise level of the block being denoised. Specifically, the history is the K/V produced by preceding blocks at the same denoising stage, so all blocks at a stage can be denoised in a single forward pass under a causal mask. This keeps the noisy history differentiable, allowing future losses to optimize how it is encoded. SAF therefore avoids a separate no-gradient rollout and per-block timestep-zero recaching, training up to 1.8x faster than prior methods with lower memory. At inference, SAF achieves the highest single-GPU throughput among existing methods and keeps one history bank per stage for a multi-GPU pipeline, reaching 49.1 FPS on 4 GPUs. Experiments show superior long-horizon generation with a better balance between visual quality and motion. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [218] [GA-EIRFS: A Geometry-Augmented Repeat-Factor Sampling Method for Long-Tailed LiDAR 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38116)
*Taufiq Ahmed,Constantino Álvarez Casado,Daniel Herrera Castro,Sasan Sharifipour,Abhishek Kumar,Miguel Bordallo López*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GA-EIRFS采样方法，用几何可观测性分数调制长尾3D检测的频率重复因子，仅改采样概率即可提升nuScenes检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾3D检测通常被当作类频率问题处理，但LiDAR监督质量实际取决于物体的几何可观测性：相同频率的类别可能隐藏完全不同的几何证据量，因此纯频率驱动的重采样忽略了监督信号的可得性差异。

Method: GA-EIRFS（几何增强指数加权实例感知重复因子采样）：在基于频率的repeat factor基础上，乘以一个固定的几何分数，该分数由点数、表面法向熵和表面覆盖率组合而成。方法仅改变帧采样概率，检测器结构与推理流程不变，因而与检测器无关。

Result: 在nuScenes上，用CenterPoint和PointPillars、两个随机种子共四次收敛实验，mAP与NDS均提升；CenterPoint（seed 666）mAP从0.552升至0.563，bicycle AP从0.306升至0.359。类别增益与采样权重增幅相关（Spearman rho=0.70, p=0.025），但与单独几何分数不相关（rho=0.32, p=0.37）。KITTI上结果随种子波动，最稀有类别波动最大。

Conclusion: 几何可观测性对频率驱动的采样需求起放大作用而非独立驱动作用；方法低成本、检测器无关，但对KITTI等数据集稳定性有限，收益依赖频率-几何的耦合。

Abstract: Long-tailed 3D object detection is treated as a class-frequency problem, but LiDAR supervision quality depends on object observability: similar frequencies can hide different geometric evidence. We introduce Geometry-Augmented Exponentially Weighted Instance-Aware Repeat Factor Sampling (GA-EIRFS), a detector-agnostic method that modulates a frequency-based repeat factor with a fixed geometry score combining point count, surface-normal entropy, and surface coverage. GA-EIRFS changes only frame-sampling probabilities, leaving the detector and inference unchanged. On nuScenes it improves mean average precision (mAP) and the nuScenes detection score (NDS) in four converged experiments with CenterPoint and PointPillars over two seeds; for CenterPoint at seed 666, mAP rises from 0.552 to 0.563 and bicycle AP from 0.306 to 0.359. Per-class gains correlate with the class sampling-weight increase (Spearman rho=0.70, p=0.025) but not with geometry score alone (rho=0.32, p=0.37), so geometry amplifies frequency-driven need. KITTI results vary across seeds, most for the rarest class. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [219] [VideoLoop: Looped Working Memory Against Semantic Thrashing in Long-Form Video Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38119)
*Jinfa Huang,Jianming Xu,Jingyang Lin,Zhengyuan Yang,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对长视频理解中智能体"语义抖动"问题，作者论证仅追加式记忆无法删除噪声，提出双循环智能体 VideoLoop：外循环推理，内循环从无界文件系统检索并重写有界工作记忆，在多个 LVLM 骨干上即插即用提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解需要多模态智能体进行多步迭代证据收集。现有方法多采用仅追加（append-only）工作记忆，随着上下文增长，对关键证据的注意力崩塌，智能体"遗忘"已找到的信息，即所谓语义抖动（semantic thrashing）。作者希望从结构上论证该缺陷并给出解决方案。

Method: 首先给出结构性论证：仅追加式记忆可以纳入新观测的目标证据，但无法删除累积的噪声，也无法在不引入重写算子的情况下阻止有序上下文无限增长。基于此提出 VideoLoop：一个具有两个耦合循环的多模态智能体。外循环对视频进行推理；内循环在每一步之后，从无界文件系统（存储过往观测和中间分析产物）中检索相关片段，并重写有界的工作记忆，从而保持上下文紧凑且聚焦。

Result: 可即插即用地改进四个主流 LVLM 骨干，在 VideoMME (long) 上平均较基线提升 4.2 个百分点。工作记忆分析显示确实缓解了语义抖动：在 VideoMME (long) 最难的四分之一问题上，只读取智能体上下文的盲判官正确率为 81.1%，而仅追加式智能体为 60.9%。配合 Gemini 3.1 Pro，VideoLoop 在 VideoMME (long) 达 88.3%，VideoMMMU 达 88.8%，LongVideoBench (long) 达 80.9%。

Conclusion: 仅追加式工作记忆受限于无法删除噪声与限制上下文增长，存在语义抖动；通过引入检索+重写的有界工作记忆的双循环智能体设计可有效缓解该问题，并在多个长视频理解基准上取得一致提升，且与多种 LVLM 骨干兼容。

Abstract: Long-form video understanding requires multimodal agents to iteratively gather evidence over many reasoning steps. However, most existing agentic methods suffer from semantic thrashing: as append-only working memory grows, attention to key evidence collapses, and the agent loses access to what it has already found. First, we provide a structural argument showing that append-only memory can incorporate newly observed target evidence, but cannot remove accumulated noise or prevent ordered context growth without a rewrite operator. Second, motivated by this analysis, we propose VideoLoop, a multimodal agent with two coupled loops. The outer loop reasons over the video and the inner loop, after each step, retrieves artifacts from an unbounded filesystem of past observations and intermediate analysis, and rewrites a bounded working memory. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of VideoLoop, which improves four popular LVLM backbones in a plug-and-play manner, with an average gain of 4.2% points over baseline on VideoMME (long). Further analysis of working memory suggests that VideoLoop mitigates semantic thrashing: on the hardest quarter of VideoMME (long) questions, a blind judge that reads only the agent's context answers 81.1% correctly, versus 60.9% for the append-only agent. With Gemini 3.1 Pro, VideoLoop reaches 88.3% on VideoMME (long), 88.8% on VideoMMMU, and 80.9% on LongVideoBench (long).

</details>


### [220] [HelixWorld: A Real-time Interactive Audio-Visual World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38123)
*Lei Ke,Jiahao Pan,Zeyue Tian,Jiaming Wang,Haoyuan Huang,Kam Man Wu,Pengjun Fang,Hongyu Liu,Chenyang Qi,Lin Wang,Ruibin Yuan,Weijia Chen,Fangneng Zhan,Qifeng Chen,Wei Xue,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HelixWorld：实时交互式音频-视觉世界模型，视觉场景与相机锚定的空间立体声在用户交互下联合演化；双向教师+在线轨迹蒸馏的少步流式学生，单GPU 24FPS无漂移联合rollout，并提出空间声学一致性形式化与HelixBench评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式世界模型仅做视觉渲染与控制，普遍"静音"，忽略声学维度；而真实世界模拟本质上是多感官的，需要视觉与声学动力学实时同步。因此需要一个原生联合音频-视觉、支持6-DoF相机与用户动作的实时交互式世界模型。

Method: 构建含真实双声道声学与米制相机位姿的高保真空间音视频数据集；在该数据上预训练以6-DoF相机轨迹和用户动作为条件的双向教师模型；再通过在线轨迹蒸馏损失，将教师蒸馏为少步流式学生模型，实现低延迟因果交互；同时形式化空间-声学一致性并构建HelixBench评测套件。

Result: 在单GPU上以24 FPS维持无漂移的联合音视频rollout；视觉保真度与响应性与当前最先进的"静音"世界模型相当，而在相机对齐的空间声学沉浸感上显著超越现有基线。

Conclusion: HelixWorld表明世界模型可以在保持视觉质量与实时性的同时原生引入空间声学维度，为多感官交互式世界模拟提供了可行范式与配套评测标准。

Abstract: World simulation is inherently multisensory, demanding synchronized visual and acoustic dynamics in real time. Yet prevailing interactive world models remain strictly silent, focusing exclusively on visual rendering and control while overlooking the acoustic dimension. We present HelixWorld, a real-time interactive audio-visual world model where visual scenes and camera-grounded spatial stereo sound co-evolve natively under user interaction. We curate a high-fidelity spatial audio-visual dataset with true stereo acoustics and metric camera poses, upon which we pre-train a bidirectional teacher conditioned on 6-DoF camera trajectories and user actions. To enable low-latency causal interaction, we distill the teacher into a few-step streaming student via an online trajectory distillation loss, sustaining drift-free joint audio-visual rollouts at 24 FPS on a single GPU. Furthermore, we formalize spatial-acoustic consistency and introduce HelixBench to evaluate whether synthesized sound fields faithfully track dynamic viewpoint motion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HelixWorld matches state-of-the-art silent world models in visual fidelity and responsiveness, while significantly surpassing existing baselines in camera-aligned spatial-acoustic immersion.

</details>


### [221] [CLeaR: A Unified Framework for Resolving the Leakage-Degradation Dilemma in Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38136)
*Teng Zhou,Yunhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练风格迁移框架 CLeaR，通过正交子空间投影、集成反演与能量引导校准，在保持风格保真度的同时抑制内容泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风格迁移方法存在"内容泄漏"问题：参考图像中的物体、布局或语义会窜入生成结果。此前的数据驱动与免训练方法虽能缓解泄漏，却陷入"泄漏—退化两难"：抑制内容往往削弱风格保真，强化风格又引入参考内容。作者进一步将这一困境定位到整个流程（特征分离、特征空间锚定、扩散生成）中的多个环节。

Method: 免训练框架 CLeaR，三步：（1）正交子空间投影（Orthogonal Subspace Projection），在每个视觉基础模型（VFM）特征空间中构造"内容削减"的风格目标；（2）集成反演（Ensemble Inversion），在像素空间优化一个共享的风格锚点，使其同时满足多个 VFM 的风格约束；（3）能量引导校准（Energy-Guided Calibration），在扩散采样过程中将去噪轨迹导向集成定义的风格流形。此外给出理论分析，证明风格锚点估计误差随 VFM 数量增加而下降。

Result: 在 StyleBench 上，相较现有方法，CLeaR 提升了风格对齐度、降低了内容泄漏，并在 LLM-as-Judge 评测中取得更好表现；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 将内容泄漏问题系统性归因于完整风格迁移流程，并用免训练的多 VFM 集成与投影/校准机制加以解决，二者权衡得到缓解。

Abstract: Style transfer aims to render target content in the style of a reference image, but existing methods often suffer from content leakage, where objects, layouts, or semantics from the style reference appear in the generated output. Although prior data-driven and training-free methods can reduce leakage, they often face a leakage-degradation dilemma: stronger content suppression may weaken style fidelity, while richer style preservation may reintroduce unwanted reference content. We identify this dilemma across the full style-transfer pipeline, including feature separation, feature-space grounding, and diffusion generation. To address these issues, we propose CLeaR, a training-free framework for content-leakage-resistant style transfer. CLeaR first uses Orthogonal Subspace Projection to define content-reduced style targets in each vision foundation model (VFM) feature space. It then performs Ensemble Inversion, which optimizes a shared pixel-space style anchor satisfying style constraints across multiple VFMs. Finally, Energy-Guided Calibration maintains style alignment during diffusion sampling by steering the denoising trajectory toward the ensemble-defined style manifold. We further provide a theoretical analysis showing that the style-anchor estimation error decreases with the number of VFMs. Experiments on StyleBench demonstrate that CLeaR improves style alignment, reduces content leakage, and achieves better LLM-as-Judge evaluation compared with existing methods. The code is available at \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [222] [Breaking the Uniformity Trap: Scaling Video Diffusion Model via SplitMoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38140)
*Yu Xu,Yuxin Zhang,Xiao Yang,Haotian Yang,Yizhi Wang,Xinwei Huang,Minxuan Lin,Angtian Wang,Chongyang Ma,Fan Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplitMoE 提出分角色稀疏专家架构，将专家池显式拆分为语义专家与通用专家，配合原型引导路由与拉推正则，打破视觉 MoE 的"均匀性陷阱"，在等效激活参数预算下提升视频生成收敛速度、路由一致性与生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 token-wise MoE 独立路由 token 并强制专家使用均匀化，与视频数据时空冗余、语义长尾的特性不匹配，导致路由碎片化与结构失真。作者将这一现象归纳为"均匀性陷阱"。

Method: 提出 SplitMoE：将专家池二分为语义专家（高层语义抽象）与通用专家（保留残差视觉信息与生成柔性）；采用原型引导路由（prototype-guided routing）与拉推正则（pull-push regularization），使 token 依语义属性自然聚类，而非受人为均衡约束。

Result: 在等效激活参数预算下，SplitMoE 在收敛速度、路由一致性与标准基准上的视频生成质量均优于传统负载均衡 MoE，并观察到涌现的由粗到细去噪逻辑。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了视觉 MoE 的均匀性陷阱，为视觉生成模型提供了模态感知的稀疏扩展路径，可作为大规模视频世界模型构建的参考。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), popularized by large language models, is a promising paradigm for scaling visual generative models. However, conventional token-wise MoE routes tokens independently within a homogeneous expert pool and regularizes expert usage toward uniformity, making it poorly matched to video data that is spatiotemporally redundant and semantically long-tailed. We show that existing visual MoEs fall into a uniformity trap: semantically under-organized routing, compounded by uniform expert-usage regularization, scatters coherent patches across disparate experts, causing routing fragmentation and structural distortion. To address this, we propose SplitMoE, a split-role sparse architecture that breaks the shackles of uniformity. To accommodate the inherent semantic imbalance, we explicitly bifurcate the expert pool into semantic experts and generic experts, with semantic experts capturing high-level semantic abstraction and generic experts preserving residual visual information and flexible generative capacity. Leveraging prototype-guided routing and pull-push regularization, SplitMoE enables tokens to cluster naturally by semantic attributes rather than arbitrary balancing constraints. Extensive results show that under an equivalent activated-parameter budget, SplitMoE outperforms traditional load-balanced MoEs in convergence speed, routing coherence, and video generation quality across standard benchmarks. By revealing an emergent coarse-to-fine denoising logic, SplitMoE provides the community with a modality-aware scaling path, serving as a critical reference for building large-scale video world models.

</details>


### [223] [LIFT: Layout-In-Future Video Generation under Large Viewpoint Change via On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38146)
*Shengxiang Ji,Boyang Wang,Haiyang Xu,Bingnan Li,Yucheng Mao,Zeyuan Chen,Xiaojun Shan,Xiang Zhang,Gang Hua,Jianwen Xie,Zezhou Cheng,Zhuowen Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LIFT 提出统一图生视频框架，以「末帧布局」作为未来视图的显式控制信号，并借助 on-policy self-distillation 将稠密逐帧布局教师的控制能力迁移到仅末帧布局的学生模型，配套 LIFT-Vista 数据集，提升视频质量与布局/相机可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可控视频生成中，给定首帧后用户不仅关心相机如何运动，还关心未来关键时刻（尤其末帧）场景内容与空间布局。现有相机控制仅给出视角轨迹，文本提示仅提供粗粒度语义，均无法精确决定未来视图的内容与空间排布；大视角变化下相机揭示首帧不可见区域时该问题尤为突出。

Method: 将末帧布局作为期望未来场景的显式控制条件；由于从稀疏布局监督中学习远难于逐帧稠密布局条件，引入 on-policy self-distillation（OPSD），把稠密布局教师（teacher）的控制能力蒸馏到末帧布局学生（student）；并构建 LIFT-Vista 数据集，包含大视角变化且相机与时间一致的布局标注。

Result: 实验表明 LIFT 在视频质量、未来布局可控性与相机可控性上均优于其他方法。

Conclusion: 以末帧布局为显式控制信号并用 OPSD 弥补稀疏监督的学习难度，可行地实现了「指定未来视图内容与位置」的图生视频生成；但摘要未给出定量指标、开放代码或真实部署证据。

Abstract: We introduce LIFT, a unified image-to-video generation framework that complements camera control with Layout-In-FuTure control, enabling users to specify what should appear in a future view and where it should appear. This addresses a practical need in controllable video generation: given an initial image, users often care not only about how the camera moves, but also about what the scene should look like at key future moments, especially the final frame. Existing camera controls specify viewpoint trajectories, while text prompts provide only coarse semantic guidance; neither precisely determines the content and spatial layout of future views. This limitation becomes particularly pronounced under large viewpoint changes, where the camera reveals regions that are not visible in the first frame. LIFT therefore uses the last-frame layout as an explicit control signal for the desired future scene. Since learning from such sparse layout guidance is substantially more challenging than conditioning on dense per-frame layouts, we introduce on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) to transfer the control capability of a dense-layout teacher to a last-frame-layout student. We further curate LIFT-Vista, a dataset featuring large viewpoint changes with camera and temporally consistent layout annotations. Experiments show that LIFT improves video quality, future-layout controllability, and camera controllability over other methods.

</details>


### [224] [FracGen: Learning How Objects Stretch and Tear with Physics-Informed Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38152)
*Trong-Tung Nguyen,Jiahan Zhang,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FracGen——物理信号可控的断裂感知视频生成模型，并构建带连续损伤模型的 MPM 模拟器 FracSim 生成配对数据与像素对齐物理场，配合物理一致性损失与断裂合理性基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型难以刻画材料断裂这类强非线性、依赖物理状态的动态过程：既缺少带物理标注的大规模断裂数据，也缺乏显式的物理约束机制，导致生成结果停留于表观纹理而无法正确表达裂纹扩展与失效前变形。

Method: 1) FracSim：在物质点法（MPM）上叠加连续介质损伤模型，以标准渲染成本额外产出配对断裂视频与稠密、像素对齐的物理场（如损伤、速度、应力等）；2) FracGen：以单张完整物体图像加物理信号为条件，联合预测 RGB 视频与物理场图，使模型学习物理状态而非表面外观；3) 引入物理知情损失，约束各预测物理场之间的一致性；4) 构建断裂视频物理合理性评估基准。

Result: 无需测试时昂贵的物理模拟或逐场景调参即可复现材料特异的断裂行为，并可细粒度控制破裂位置、裂纹扩展速度与失效前变形幅度；在物理合理性与视觉保真度两类指标上均优于现有视频生成基线。

Conclusion: 将可微/显式物理信号引入生成模型的条件与监督环节，是提升生成视频物理可信度的有效途径；同时提供的数据生成管线与评测基准为该方向后续研究提供了可复用工具。当前证据以模拟数据与指标评测为主，尚未验证真实世界泛化与下游操作任务。

Abstract: We introduce FracGen, a fracture-aware video generation model that produces plausible, controllable fracture dynamics from a single image of an intact object, conditioned on physics signals. To train FracGen, we build FracSim, a fracture-aware simulation framework that augments material point method (MPM) simulation with a continuum damage model, producing paired fracture videos and dense, pixel-aligned physical fields at no additional cost beyond standard rendering. FracGen leverages these maps in two ways: it is trained to jointly predict them alongside RGB video, encouraging the model to capture physical state rather than surface appearance; and it is supervised with physics-informed losses that encourage consistency among the predicted maps. As a result, FracGen captures distinct material-specific fracture behavior without expensive test-time simulation or per-scene tuning, while offering fine-grained control over where an object tears, how fast the crack propagates, and how much deformation precedes failure. We further introduce a benchmark for evaluating the physical plausibility of generated fracture video, and show through extensive experiments that FracGen outperforms existing video generation baselines in both physical and visual fidelity. Results are best viewed in our project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [225] [PowerSim: Differentiable Physics Simulation and Rendering with Power Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38153)
*Trong-Tung Nguyen,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PowerSim 将预训练的 PowerFoam（power diagram 3D 表示）与物质点法（MPM）直接耦合，利用基元几何/外观属性与 MPM 追踪量的天然对齐，使仿真可直接驱动场景几何与外观，无需中间表示；实现用户交互仿真、空间变化材质场恢复、跨场景基元合成以及随形变一致更新的光线追踪反射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理仿真框架与神经/基元式 3D 表示之间往往需要辅助表示做桥接，导致几何与外观无法被仿真结果直接驱动，且难以在动态场景中保持反射等二次光照一致性。作者希望把物理可微动力学"内嵌"进 power diagram 表示，实现物理与外观统一驱动。

Method: 核心观察：PowerFoam 每个基元的几何与外观属性与 MPM 在形变过程中已追踪的物理量高度对应，因此可直接建立耦合，无需 auxiliary representation。在此框架上构建四类应用：静态场景的用户交互仿真、空间变化材质场反演、将独立采集场景的基元合成为单一可仿真场景、以及随形变一致更新的光线追踪反射（二次光线光照效果）。

Result: 在真实与合成场景上验证了上述四类应用；作者称 PowerSim 在物理基础动力学方面优于既有框架，并带来独特优势，例如动态场景上的二次光线光照效果。定量结果需参见项目网站。

Conclusion: PowerSim 表明将物理仿真与 power diagram 3D 表示直接对齐耦合是一种有效路径，可同时支持交互仿真、材质反演、场景合成与动态反射渲染；但其"优于既有框架"的结论目前仅由摘要陈述支撑，缺乏具体指标与代码/部署信息。

Abstract: We introduce PowerSim, a method to bring physically grounded, differentiable dynamics to PowerFoam's power diagram based 3D representation. PowerSim directly couples a pre-trained PowerFoam scene to the Material Point Method (MPM) by exploiting a natural alignment between the two: the geometric and appearance properties of each primitive correspond closely to the quantities MPM already tracks as an object deforms. Consequently, simulated motion can drive the scene's geometry and appearance directly, without an auxiliary representation in between. Built on this framework, we enable a range of applications on real and synthetic scenes: (1) simulating a static scene under user interaction, (2) recovering spatially varying material fields, (3) compositing primitives from independently captured scenes into a single simulation-ready scene and (4) ray-tracing reflections that update consistently as the object deforms. Our results suggest that PowerSim excels over previous frameworks for physically grounded dynamics, while unlocking unique advantages-such as secondary ray lighting effects on dynamic scenes. Results are best viewed on our project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [226] [LongLive-Plug: Once-for-All Distillation for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38154)
*Shuai Yang,Luozhou Wang,Wei Huang,ZhiFei Chen,Bohan Zhang,Xiao Fu,Qianli Ma,Chen-Hsuan Lin,Weian Mao,Bryan Chu,Song Han,Yukang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LongLive-Plug一次性蒸馏框架，将单遍CFG、少步采样、长上下文纠错等能力蒸馏为可复用LoRA，无需重训练即可插拔部署到54个下游视频扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型常针对各下游任务专门化，且每个专门化模型都需重复蒸馏（加速采样或改进长视频生成），成本高、不可复用，缺乏一种跨模型共享的蒸馏方案。

Method: 在基础模型上以LoRA形式学习可复用能力（单遍classifier-free guidance、少步采样、自回归长上下文误差校正），使adapter在目标模型新增条件分支或扩展输出通道后仍可复用；通过推理时权重调节实现CFG控制，并与少步LoRA组合使用。

Result: 在3个骨干家族、8类任务（世界建模、机器人、编辑、多模态生成等）共54个下游模型上验证了免训练部署，单一能力按骨干家族蒸馏一次即可复用，无需逐目标重训练。

Conclusion: 一次性蒸馏+可插拔LoRA为视频扩散模型的专门化部署提供了高效路径，具备跨模型、跨任务的泛化能力，可扩展至更多兼容模型。

Abstract: Video diffusion models are increasingly developed into specialized models for diverse downstream tasks, and this development often includes a distillation stage, for example to accelerate sampling or to improve long-video generation. This stage is typically repeated for every specialized model. We introduce LongLive-Plug, a once-for-all distillation framework that learns reusable capabilities as LoRAs on a base model for training-free, plug-and-play deployment to compatible downstream models. These capabilities include single-pass classifier-free guidance, few-step sampling, and long-context error correction for autoregressive generation. The adapters remain reusable even when downstream models add conditioning branches, expand output channels. Despite training at a fixed guidance scale, our dedicated CFG LoRA provides text guidance control through its inference weight. Combining it with a few-step LoRA simultaneously preserves few-step generation and CFG controllability on downstream tasks. We verify training-free deployment on 54 downstream models across three backbone families and eight task categories, including world modeling, robotics, editing, and multimodal generation. The approach may support additional compatible models. Each capability can thus be distilled once per backbone family and reused without per-target retraining.

</details>


### [227] [Beyond the Timeline: Augmenting Long-Video Memory with Grounded Entity Biographies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38155)
*Hui Ren,Lei Fan,Henry Pao,Han Guo,Zeeshan Zia,Ying Chen,Alexander Schwing,Gang Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Grounded Entity Biographies (GEB) 长视频记忆框架：将跨片段中同一物理实例的视觉接地观测聚合成可检索的"实体传记"，并在问答时与情节证据联合检索，实现基于身份链接的实体级事件追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答需跨小时甚至跨天连接同一物体的相关事件，但按时间顺序的描述与文本衍生实体无法确定物理身份：不同物体可能共享描述，而同一物体的观测却被割裂在事件之间。因此仅检索相关事件并不等于恢复问题所指实体的"传记"。

Method: 构建长视频记忆框架 GEB，在记忆构建阶段将视觉接地（grounded）的同一物理实例观测跨片段分组为可检索的 biography，同时保留每个时刻的上下文；问答阶段将 biography 与 episodic evidence 一同检索，使模型能依据记忆构建时建立的身份链接追踪实体。

Result: 在四个基准（含全天与整周录像）上，多选与开放式问答均优于既有记忆框架；EgoLifeQA 达到 72.0% 准确率，比已公开最好结果高 4.4 个百分点。消融显示接地身份关联与传记读取均有贡献，且仅靠额外描述无法完全弥补。

Conclusion: 视觉接地的实体身份关联可解决长视频中物理身份未解析的问题，身份传记式记忆检索是提升长时程问答的有效范式。

Abstract: Answering questions about long videos often requires connecting events involving the same objects across hours or days. Chronological descriptions and text-derived entities can leave physical identity unresolved: different objects may share a description, while observations of the same object remain disconnected across events. Retrieving relevant events therefore does not necessarily recover the "biography" of the particular entity a question concerns. To address this, we introduce Grounded Entity Biographies (GEB), a long-video memory framework that groups visually grounded observations of the same physical instance across clips into retrievable biographies while preserving the context of each moment. During question answering, the biography is retrieved alongside episodic evidence, allowing the model to follow an entity through events using identity links established during memory construction. Evaluations across four benchmarks, including day-long and week-long recordings, demonstrate improvements over prior memory frameworks in both multiple-choice and open-ended question answering. On EgoLifeQA, GEB achieves 72.0% accuracy, 4.4 percentage points above the best published result. Ablations show that grounded identity association and biography reading both contribute to the gains, which additional descriptions alone do not fully recover.

</details>


### [228] [DMA$^2$: Pixel-space Distribution Matching with Adversarial and Anchor Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38156)
*Xin Lin,Zhifei Zhang,Yuqian Zhou,Haitian Zheng,Shaoteng Liu,Lehan Yang,Zhe Lin,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出DMA²框架，将分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）从潜空间推广到原生RGB像素空间，用于少步文本到图像生成，四步学生模型超越25步教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代DMD的文本到图像实现主要基于潜空间扩散，忽略了原生RGB的性质与设计机会。作者希望重新审视像素空间教师的DMD接口，利用原生RGB的清洁输出和外部视觉表示来改进少步蒸馏。

Method: 工作重新审视了两个像素空间教师的DMD接口：（1）教师匹配侧，诊断发现低噪声RGB匹配被局部纹理线索主导，故采用固定的高噪声匹配带；（2）真实数据侧，利用原生清洁RGB输出，引入DINO-Adv，从对抗梯度路径中移除重型假分数评论器，提供局部参数化patch引导。此外提出AF-Loss，一种参数无关的辅助语义分布场目标，在共享DINOv2空间中作用于分离的滚动真实与生成支撑集，同时保留提示条件教师监督。AF-Loss不含可学习参数，也不增加推理计算。上述设计共同构成DMA²。

Result: 在DPG-Bench、GenEval、VQAScore和COCO30K上，四步DMA²学生模型优于25步教师模型及所评估的少步蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: DMA²表明原生RGB空间为DMD提供了被忽视的设计空间，通过高噪声匹配带、DINO-Adv局部引导和无参数AF-Loss语义分布场目标，可实现高效的少步文本到图像蒸馏。

Abstract: Distribution matching distillation (DMD) provides a general framework for few-step diffusion generation, but its modern text-to-image instantiations have been developed primarily around latent diffusion. It therefore overlooks key properties and design opportunities of native RGB. We revisit two DMD interfaces for pixel-space teachers. On the teacher-matching side, diagnostics show low-noise RGB matching is dominated by a local-texture cue, motivating a fixed high-noise matching band. On the real-data side, native clean-RGB outputs allow guidance from an external visual representation without traversing a decoder or sharing the heavy fake-score critic. DINO-Adv removes this critic from the adversarial gradient path and supplies local parametric patch guidance. For distribution-level guidance, we introduce AF-Loss, a parameter-free auxiliary semantic distribution-field objective designed for text-to-image DMD. It operates on detached rolling real and generated supports in the shared DINOv2 space while preserving prompt-conditioned teacher supervision. AF-Loss adds no learnable parameters or inference-time computation. Together these designs form DMA$^2$. Across DPG-Bench, GenEval, VQAScore, and COCO30K, the four-step DMA$^2$ student performs better than the 25-step teacher and evaluated few-step distillers.

</details>


### [229] [Rethinking Representations for World-Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38163)
*Haoyi Jiang,Liu Liu,Xinjiang Wang,Zhihao Sun,Zequn Chen,Sen Wang,Xinjie Wang,Xia Chen,Jingfeng Yao,Weiheng Zhao,Shanglin Yuan,Zhizhong Su,Wei Sui,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReWAM：以预训练 DINO 特征为核心表征的世界-动作模型（WAM），通过特征校准、时序表征瓶颈与动作锚定表征塑造，分离"策略塑造表征内容"与"世界模型学习表征演化"，无需生成式视频预训练即在 RoboTwin 2.0 达 93.6% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型需同时学策略与预测未来观测，其表征空间是控制与预测的接口。但现有设计未明确该空间应如何构造：仅靠重建保真度或纯预训练感知特征都不足以保证策略学习效果，需要系统性对比研究来确定有效表征的组织方式。

Method: 通过受控对比实验分析表征设计（重建保真 vs. 预训练感知特征），据此构建 ReWAM：以冻结 DINO 特征为基座；用特征校准（Feature Calibration）与时序表征瓶颈将其压缩为适配动力学建模的紧凑世界状态；用动作锚定表征塑造（Action-Grounded Representation Shaping）仅将动作损失梯度回传至瓶颈，使策略决定表征编码内容、世界模型专注学习其演化。

Result: 在无生成式视频预训练条件下，RoboTwin 2.0 达 93.6% 成功率；RoboDojo 上使用约 600 小时具身预训练数据，平均得分 12.29、成功率 8.28%。

Conclusion: 世界-动作模型的表征空间需面向动力学与控制联合设计，而非单纯追求重建质量或直接复用感知特征；梯度路由式的解耦（策略塑形内容、世界模型学习演化）是一种有效且无需生成式视频预训练的可行范式。

Abstract: World-action models jointly learn robot policies and predict future observations, making the representation space an interface between control and prediction. We study the design of this space through controlled comparisons, finding that neither reconstruction fidelity nor pre-trained perceptual features alone ensure effective policy learning. These findings motivate ReWAM, a representation-centric world-action model built on pre-trained DINO features. Feature Calibration and a Temporal Representation Bottleneck organize these features into compact world states suited to dynamics modeling. Action-Grounded Representation Shaping routes only action-loss gradients to the bottleneck, thereby letting the policy shape what the representation encodes while the world model learns how it evolves. Without generative video pre-training, ReWAM achieves 93.6% success on RoboTwin 2.0. On RoboDojo, it achieves an average score of 12.29 and a success rate of 8.28% using approximately 600 hours of embodied pre-training data.

</details>


### [230] [Cropland PAtteRNS: Parallel Dimensional Attention Networks and Attention to Dataset Disparity for Crop Segmentation in Satellite Imagery Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38165)
*Joseph Metcalfe,Sara Sharifzadeh,Fabio Caraffini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出混合Transformer-卷积模型PAtteRNS，首次对Sentinel-2多光谱SITS数据的时间、光谱、空间三个维度分别做自注意力，并用并行Transformer架构降低三因子化自注意力的计算复杂度，在PASTIS和MTLCC数据集上取得SOTA作物分割性能，同时揭示数据集类别分组缺陷与瓦片尺寸变体不可比的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感SITS数据集与分割架构日益丰富，但研究热潮偏向追求最新概念和最大数据集，忽视了细节问题：缺乏对时间、光谱、空间多维信息的完全因子化建模，且数据集构建规范不统一（类别分组粗糙、瓦片尺寸变体导致结果不可比），影响公平评估与实际分割质量（尤其地块边界划分质量常被低估）。

Method: 提出Cropland PAtteRNS混合Transformer-卷积模型：对Sentinel-2多光谱SITS数据的时间、光谱、空间三个维度分别施加自注意力，实现完全因子化注意力；设计新型并行Transformer架构以显著降低三重因子化自注意力的计算复杂度。通过详细消融实验验证架构，并在PASTIS与MTLCC的多种瓦片尺寸变体上与SOTA作物分割模型对比，采用多种分割指标评估，其中特别使用Boundary IoU衡量地块边界划分质量。

Result: 在作物类别分割任务上，PAtteRNS在多种分割指标上均优于所有对比模型；在通常报告较少的边界划分质量（Boundary IoU）上优势尤为明显。此外发现：数据集内错误的类别分组会显著损害模型性能；不同瓦片尺寸的作物分割数据集变体产生的结果彼此不可比，使得在不同瓦片尺寸上训练的模型性能无法进行公平比较。

Conclusion: PAtteRNS验证了按时间、光谱、空间分别自注意力的完全因子化建模在作物分割中的有效性。作者呼吁在构建SITS作物分割数据集时标准化最佳实践，并支持未来动态瓦片尺寸以实现最优模型性能。

Abstract: The landscape of satellite imagery time series datasets and boundary-pushing architectures for cropland segmentation has never been richer. However, in this gold rush, important truths are being missed on both fronts, as a drive for the most novel concepts or the largest datasets pushes finer details to the side. In this paper, we present our hybrid transformer-convolutional model, Cropland Parallel Attention and Refinement Network for Segmentation (PAtteRNS), the first model to use self-attention mechanisms separately for each of the temporal, spectral, and spatial aspects of Sentinel-2 multispectral SITS data. To achieve fully-factorised attention in our proposed model, we introduce a novel parallel transformer architecture which significantly reduces the computational complexity of triple-factorised self-attention. We validate our architecture with an in-depth ablation study, and analyse the performance of our model against state-of-the-art crop segmentation models on multiple tile-size variants of the popular PASTIS and MTLCC datasets. Our findings show our model to outperform all others in the task of crop class segmentation, verified across multiple important segmentation metrics, with especially strong performance against compared models seen in the often under-reported parcel delineation quality, for which we use the Boundary IoU metric. We also find that flawed class groupings within datasets can have a significant negative impact on model performance, and report that alternate tile-size variants of crop segmentation datasets produce results incomparable to one-another, invalidating fair comparison between model performance when trained on different tile-sizes. Based on these findings, we suggest further work is required to standardise best practices when constructing SITS crop segmentation datasets, and to enable future dynamic-tile-sizing for ideal model performance.

</details>


### [231] [Adversarial Training for Pixel Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38170)
*Xin Lin,Zhifei Zhang,Yuqian Zhou,Haitian Zheng,Zhe Lin,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个像素扩散模型的对抗后训练系统性研究：在保留原扩散/流匹配目标的基础上，对非高噪声时间步的预测输出加入对抗损失，在不改变架构与采样流程的前提下，联合提升分布保真度、覆盖度、提示对齐和感知质量，并通过频带/幂律分析揭示其机理是恢复了缺失的高频谱功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素扩散模型直接生成RGB图像，规避了自编码器瓶颈，但其输出仍系统性地低估细尺度自然图像统计特性。作者希望以低成本后训练方式修正这一缺陷，并系统回答"为何有效"以及"何时失效"。

Method: 从预训练像素扩散模型出发，保留原有扩散或流匹配目标，仅额外在非高噪声时间步的预测输出上添加对抗损失；不改动模型架构与采样过程。对两个像素骨干网络进行验证，并通过频带与幂律分析、最近邻检索、recall、匹配的无GAN SFT对照排除记忆化、模式坍塌与单纯优化增强等解释，同时在潜空间扩散配置上检验方法边界。

Result: 在两个像素骨干上，方法同时改善分布保真度、覆盖度、提示对齐与感知质量；频谱分析显示原模型系统性缺失高频内容，对抗后训练恢复该功率，而感知损失虽也增加高频却损害分布保真度与提示对齐。在测试的潜空间扩散配置下，同一流程无类似联合增益，且几乎不增加解码后的高频功率。

Conclusion: 对抗后训练能有效修正像素扩散模型的频谱缺陷，其成功关键在于模型能否直接访问待修正的图像统计量（像素扩散可直接访问输出，潜空间扩散则受限）。

Abstract: Pixel diffusion models generate RGB images directly, avoiding the bottleneck of an autoencoder, yet their outputs still systematically underrepresent fine-scale natural-image statistics. We show that adversarial learning provides an effective post-training correction for this deficiency. Starting from a pretrained model, we retain its original diffusion or flow-matching objective and add an adversarial loss to the predicted output at non-high-noise timesteps, leaving the model architecture and sampling procedure unchanged. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of adversarial post-training for pixel diffusion. Across two pixel backbones, the method jointly improves distribution fidelity, coverage, prompt alignment, and perceptual quality. We further investigate why it works. Frequency-band and power-law analyses show that the original models systematically underproduce natural-image high-frequency content, while adversarial post-training restores this missing spectral power. In contrast, perceptual loss also increases high-frequency content but sacrifices distribution fidelity and prompt alignment. Nearest-neighbor, recall, and matched no-GAN SFT controls further rule out memorization, mode dropping, and additional optimization as simple explanations. Finally, we examine the boundary of this effect. Under the tested latent diffusion configurations, the same procedure does not produce comparable joint gains and adds almost no decoded high-frequency power. These results identify direct output access to the image statistics being corrected as a key factor governing when adversarial post-training succeeds.

</details>


### [232] [Imagine3D-LLM: Teaching MLLMs to Imagine 3D Scenes Before Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38177)
*Jaewoo Jung,Hyeonseo Yu,Honggyu An,Jisang Han,Mungyeom Kim,Minkyeong Jeon,Heeseong Shin,Wonjun Moon,Federico Tombari,Daniel Barath,Marc Pollefeys,Seungryong Kim,Sunghwan Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Imagine3D-LLM 通过可学习摘要 token 解码出紧凑 3D 高斯泼溅表示，并用光度重建损失监督，与下一词预测联合训练，从而将粗粒度 3D 场景想象注入多模态大模型以提升多视角空间推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型擅长单图理解，却难以整合多视角证据形成连贯 3D 理解。已有方法或强化像素级跨视图对应、或融合 3D 几何基础模型特征，但与人推理仍有明显差距。作者从人类空间推理获得启发：人类并不依赖细粒度几何线索，而是粗略识别跨视图共同物体、推断视角间相对几何、组装粗粒度场景 3D 布局。

Method: 在图像 token 后附加少量可学习摘要 token，将其解码为紧凑的 3D 高斯泼溅表示，以光度重建损失监督，并与标准下一词预测目标联合训练。仅摘要 token 接受直接重建监督，但该目标会反向促使 LLM 底层图像特征具备更强的跨帧对应能力，说明"学习重建"将 3D 感知信号传播至整个模型。

Result: 在多个空间推理与 3D 理解基准上，Imagine3D-LLM 一致优于此前方法；摘要 token 直接受重建监督、却连带提升图像特征的跨帧对应，验证了 3D 感知信号在模型内的传播效应。

Conclusion: 让模型"想象"场景（自监督重建紧凑 3D 表示）比直接输入像素级几何更有效，为 MLLM 的 3D 空间推理提供了一条以粗粒度重建为条件的新范式。

Abstract: Reasoning about the 3D world from multi-view images remains a fundamental challenge for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). While modern MLLMs handle single-image inputs effectively, they struggle to integrate evidence across viewpoints into a coherent 3D understanding. A growing body of work attempts to close this gap by injecting 3D awareness into MLLMs, either by boosting fine-grained pixel-level cross-view correspondence or by fusing features from 3D geometry foundation models, yet a substantial gap to human reasoning persists. In this work, we revisit human spatial reasoning, which suggests that rather than relying on fine-grained geometry cues, humans roughly identify common objects across views, infer the relative geometry between viewpoints, and assemble a coarse 3D layout of the scene. Inspired by this process, we introduce Imagine3D-LLM, an MLLM that learns to assemble a similar compact 3D representation of the scene and conditions its answer on this representation. Concretely, we append a small set of learnable summary tokens after the image tokens, decode them into a compact 3D Gaussian Splatting representation supervised by a photometric reconstruction loss, and train jointly with the standard next-token prediction objective. Notably, although only the summary tokens receive direct reconstruction supervision, this objective also induces stronger cross-frame correspondence within the LLM's underlying image features, suggesting that learning to reconstruct propagates 3D-aware signals throughout the model. As a result, Imagine3D-LLM consistently outperforms prior approaches across multiple spatial reasoning and 3D understanding benchmarks, suggesting that imagining the scene can be more effective than being told its pixel-wise geometry.

</details>


### [233] [Point2Part: Unified 3D Partitioning from Point Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38180)
*Hao-Tang Tsui,Yu-Rou Tuan,Xiaoxuan Ma,Nicolas Ugrinovic,Takaaki Shiratori,Kris Kitani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可提示（promptable）的3D部件分解模型，将整形状联合划分为互不重叠、共同覆盖全体的部件，统一支持图像/网格到部件生成与部件分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D部件分解方法允许部件重叠或存在空隙，无法构成对原始形状的完整划分，阻碍下游部件级应用；核心洞察是部件应被联合建模而非独立预测。

Method: 基于预训练3D生成模型，从图像或网格获取形状隐变量；引入提示编码器将每个3D点提示映射为部件token并注意力查询形状隐变量；提出部件解码器，以由粗到细方式对所有部件token联合评分，使形状体积内每个位置被唯一分配到一个部件；分解在共享隐空间完成，实现三类任务统一。

Result: 在三项任务（图像到部件生成、网格到部件生成、部件分割）上，部件质量指标全面超越现有方法，部件间兼容性较此前SOTA提升一个数量级。

Conclusion: 将部件分解形式化为全形状的联合划分，并在共享隐空间以点提示驱动，可获得可控、无重叠、完整覆盖的部件，统一多任务建模。

Abstract: Existing 3D part decomposition methods do not necessarily partition the original shape into non-overlapping parts that collectively cover the entire shape, allowing overlaps or gaps that hinder downstream part-level applications. We instead formulate part decomposition as a joint partitioning of the entire shape, where the predicted parts are non-overlapping and jointly recover the entire shape. Our key insight is that part decomposition should consider all desired parts jointly, rather than modeling each part independently. To this end, we develop a promptable model for 3D part decomposition from images or meshes. Users can specify desired parts through 3D point prompts for controllable decomposition. Given one point prompt per desired part, our model produces the corresponding parts as a complete partition of the entire shape. We build on a pretrained 3D generation model and first obtain a shape latent from either an input image or mesh. We then introduce a prompt encoder that maps each 3D point prompt to a part token while attending to the shape latent. To decode the desired parts, we propose a novel part decoder jointly scoring the entire shape against all part tokens in a coarse-to-fine manner, assigning every position within the shape volume to exactly one part. We perform part decomposition in this shared shape latent space, enabling a unified model for image-to-part generation, mesh-to-part generation, and part segmentation. Our method outperforms existing works on all part-quality metrics across all three tasks, and improves compatibility among parts by an order of magnitude over previous SOTA methods. Code and models will be released.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [Passive-Dynamic-Walking-Inspired Dynamics Guidance for Energy-Efficient Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35935)
*Hyeonjin Choi,Joongheon Kim,Daekyum Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出受被动动态行走启发的训练框架：早期用倾斜重力场营造"等效斜坡"条件，后期移除全部引导，在29-DoF Unitree G1上实现更经济的类人行走步态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流RL通过能耗惩罚奖励促进节能，但只是间接影响步态力学，难以发现真正机械经济（被动动力学特性）的步态协调方式。作者希望借助被动动态行走（PDW）的物理直觉直接引导节能步态的涌现，同时避免最终策略依赖人工引导。

Method: 提出PDW启发框架：早期训练用倾斜重力场在平坦碰撞几何上辅助矢状面前进以复现"等效斜坡"，配合与课程耦合的奖励项；随后在标称动力学优化前彻底移除所有PDW相关引导。框架不依赖参考轨迹、步态相位或接触时序。在29-DoF Unitree G1上做五随机种子前向运动实验，并用机械功分解与奖励匹配对照分离引导作用。

Result: 0.5–2.0 m/s指令速度下机械运输代价（CoT）降低6.8–15.2%，速度跟踪无退化；降低主要来自正功项。奖励匹配对照表明引导阶段更快获得步态，而倾斜本身对收敛后的经济性另有贡献。扩展至无辅助全向运动时，配合行走专用运动先验后速度匹配CoT降低18.7%。真机上带运动先验前向CoT降低16.3%，无先验降低4.5%（处于试验间波动范围内）。

Conclusion: 以"先引导、后撤销"的方式引入PDW物理先验，可在不损失速度跟踪的前提下提升类人行走能效，且方法可扩展到全向运动与真机部署；有运动先验时收益更稳定显著。

Abstract: Learning energy-efficient humanoid locomotion requires discovering mechanically economical gait coordination, not merely reducing actuator effort. Reinforcement learning promotes efficiency through effort-related reward penalties, which guide the step-to-step mechanics of walking only indirectly. This article proposes a framework inspired by passive dynamic walking (PDW) that temporarily creates slope-equivalent conditions favorable to economical gait discovery and removes all PDW-specific guidance before nominal-dynamics optimization. During early training, a tilted-gravity field assists sagittal progression on flat collision geometry, complemented by curriculum-coupled reward terms. The core framework requires no reference trajectories, gait phases, or contact schedules. In a five-seed forward-locomotion study on a 29-DoF Unitree G1, the framework reduces mechanical cost of transport by 6.8-15.2% over commanded speeds of 0.5-2.0m/s without degrading velocity tracking. Mechanical-work decomposition attributes the reduction to positive actuator work, and reward-matched comparisons separate the guided regime's faster gait acquisition from the tilt's additional benefit to converged economy. The framework extends to unassisted omnidirectional locomotion, where its benefit persists once a walking-specific motion prior supplies kinematic coordination, the combination reducing speed-matched cost of transport by 18.7%. On hardware, forward cost of transport falls by 16.3% with the motion prior and by 4.5% without it, the latter within the trial-to-trial spread.

</details>


### [235] [In-Context Learning for Robots: Methods and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36012)
*Haojian Huang,Zexi Li,Junhao Guo,Yehang Zhang,Wenxuan Peng,Bohan Zhou,Weilin Ruan,Leyi Wu,Chenxu Wang,Jianchong Su,Binghui Xie,Wosong Chen,Yingjie Xu,Tianhao Zhou,Suzeyu Chen,Pukun Zhao,Jiaqi He,Xinyi Li,Runze Li,Peiran Dong,Shaoxiang Dang,Jing Huang,Yingbing Chen,Yifan Chang,Tianyi Zhang,Shiyuan Deng,Haozhi Wang,Yangkai Wei,Wenqian Li,Han Yang,Kaiwen Zhou,Huaping Liu,James Cheng,Rui Shao,Donglin Wang,Yaochu Jin,Jianye Hao,Ying-Cong Chen,Yinchuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文是一篇关于机器人上下文学习（ICL）的综述，提出按"上下文证据—执行"接口划分的四大类方法框架，并给出评估与研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人需从少量演示与交互中推断新任务要求并转化为物理动作，但现有ICL研究分散，缺乏对上下文如何被利用、迁移假设如何成立以及记忆/对应关系作用的统一梳理。

Method: 综述与概念框架分析：将文献组织为四类接口——上下文条件化策略、几何演示迁移、基于世界模型的控制、技能/智能体式执行；结合操作与导航任务，比较各类接口的迁移假设及训练、对应、记忆的角色。

Result: 非实验性综述，无量化结果；产出为分类体系、跨任务（操作/导航）的机制与迁移条件分析，以及将方法设计与评估实践（教学响应性、物理迁移、经验保留收益）相连接的框架。

Conclusion: 提出将组合式任务获取与忠实迁移联系到"物理递归自我改进"的研究议程，即经验提升后续任务学习能力。

Abstract: General-purpose robots must infer what a new task requires and translate that understanding into appropriate physical action. In-context learning (ICL) for robots supports this process by using demonstrations and interaction to direct existing competence with neural parameters held fixed during deployment. We organize this literature review around the interfaces connecting contextual evidence to execution, distinguishing four families: context-conditioned policies, geometric demonstration transfer, world-model-based control, and skill- and agent-based execution.
