Result: 在完全与部分屏障下评估屏蔽行为，并与逐像素语义先验基线比较3D工作空间表示；三个家庭仿真场景中，所提风险场优化出的轨迹在同一几何约束下语义暴露低于仅碰撞约束的轨迹；同时评估了流水线的计算可行性与可靠性。

Conclusion: 该语义风险场可作为实用、拓扑感知的语义代价表示，支持超越碰撞规避的操作规划；但验证限于仿真，缺乏真机部署与代码/泛化性证据。

Abstract: Robot motion planning in everyday environments must satisfy hard geometric constraints while accounting for context-dependent semantic risk. We present a foundation-model-guided, topology-aware semantic risk field that extends manipulation safety beyond collision avoidance. For each manipulated-object/scene-object pair, a foundation model provides six directional risk weights and a pair-specific spatial decay scale. The method combines these priors with voxelized 3D scene geometry using topology-aware shielding and geodesic spatial decay. A GPU-parallel backend batches object-level distance and risk computations to construct a dense 3D field that serves as a modular cost for downstream motion planning. We evaluate the field's shielding behavior under full and partial barriers and compare its 3D workspace representation with a pixel-wise semantic-prior baseline. Across three household simulation scenarios, trajectories optimized with the proposed field have lower semantic exposure than collision-only trajectories under the same geometric constraints. We also evaluate the computational practicality and reliability of the supporting pipeline. Together, these results support the proposed field as a practical topology-aware semantic cost representation for manipulation planning beyond collision avoidance.

</details>


### [271] [Where Predictive Supervision Goes Shapes What VLA Policies Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36645)
*Hanseul Kim,Jewon Yeom,Youngjoon Jeong,Minsoo Jo,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过控制变量实验比较不同"预测接口"设计对VLA策略视觉表征的影响，发现更直接、场景匹配的未来监督能显著提升分布偏移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来预测被广泛用于改进VLA策略，其前提是"预测场景演化能促使表征更适合控制"。但预测质量本身不能证明策略学到了更好的动作表征；在分布偏移下，关键在于能否保留空间状态与场景变化信息。因此需要回答：何种预测监督才能真正改进策略用于动作的视觉表征。

Method: 在匹配目标构造、预测时域（horizon）与训练条件的前提下进行受控对比实验，系统改变预测接口（如预测目标的形式与直接性），并分析预测误差如何塑造策略的视觉流（spatial/dynamics/action 信息）。

Result: 不同预测接口产生显著不同的预测结果与视觉表征，尤其在可迁移到陌生场景的空间、动态与动作信息上差异明显；采用更直接、场景匹配的未来监督所训练的VLA策略，在模拟与真实物理分布偏移下鲁棒性更强。

Conclusion: 将未来预测视为表征学习的设计问题：其控制价值取决于监督信号能否作用于策略实际决策所依赖的表征通道。

Abstract: Future prediction is increasingly used to improve vision-language-action (VLA) policies, based on the premise that anticipating scene evolution encourages representations useful for control. However, forecast quality alone does not establish that a policy has learned a better representation for action. This distinction matters under distribution shift, where successful control depends on preserving spatial state and likely scene change beyond familiar configurations. We study what determines whether predictive supervision improves the visual representation used by a VLA policy. Through controlled comparisons with matched target constructions, prediction horizons, and training conditions, we find that different prediction interfaces produce markedly different forecasts and visual representations, including in the spatial, dynamics, and action information that transfers beyond familiar scenes. We trace these differences to how predictive errors shape the policy's visual stream. Consistent with this controlled finding, VLA policies trained with more direct, scene-matched future supervision show stronger robustness under simulated and physical distribution shifts. Together, our results frame future prediction as a representation-learning design problem whose value for control depends on whether its supervision reaches the representations through which the policy acts.

</details>


### [272] [Kinematic Nonlinear Spatio-Temporal Trajectory Warping for Contact-Rich Dexterous Manipulation Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36676)
*Hyojae Park,Arjun S. Lakshmipathy,Nancy S. Pollard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于接触分布的手-物轨迹非线性warp方法，将已有灵巧操作演示重定向到新环境、新起止构型与新时序，实现演示复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手接触丰富操作数据采集成本高、难度大，已有演示往往绑定于特定起始/终止构型、时间节奏与无遮挡场景，难以直接复用到新任务变体，因此需要一种能对现有演示做高质量轨迹变换与重定向的复用机制。

Method: 以手部与物体轨迹为输入，输出非线性轨迹warp。核心是引入接触分布（contact distribution）作为表示，使得在简单“以物体为中心”的warp规范流程下，也能可靠求解高维灵巧手轨迹；warp同时处理中间路径点、环境障碍、时间偏移以及起止构型变化。

Result: 在公开人手运动数据集上，基于4个演示构造12种变体进行评估，并进行了基线对比；同时验证方法可泛化到不同操作器（不同灵巧手/机械手）。原文声明结果与代码将在正式发表时公开。

Conclusion: 接触分布是复用灵巧操作演示的有效中间表示，能在简单物体中心warp框架下生成符合约束的高维手部轨迹，并具备跨操作器的迁移潜力；但当前证据限于人类手部运动数据集的离线变体评估，尚缺真实机器人闭环验证与公开代码。

Abstract: We present a straightforward but effective method for repurposing existing contact-rich dexterous manipulation demonstrations. Starting from inputs of hand and object trajectories, our method outputs high-quality nonlinear trajectory warps that account for intermediate waypoints, environmental barriers, temporal shifts, and varied start/end configurations. Foundational to our method is the utilization of contact distributions, which we show allows us to reliably compute complex and high-dimensional dexterous hand trajectories following a simple object-centric warp specification pipeline. We evaluate our method across 12 variations sourced from 4 demonstrations in a publicly available dataset of human hand motion data, perform baseline comparisons, and demonstrate generalization of our approach to different manipulators. Results and code will be made available on publication.

</details>


### [273] [T$^2$Mem: Learning Test-Time Memory for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36720)
*Yize Liu,Huang Huang,Yining Hong,Zijian Du,Zhi Cao,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: T²Mem：用测试时训练把观测历史压成紧凑"快权重"记忆，让无外部记忆模块的预训练VLA策略仅凭动作示范即可学会保留并利用历史信息，在16个RoboMME任务上把平均成功率从17.93%提升到56.83%，推理至少加速3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆依赖型机器人操作需要策略利用当前观测中已不存在的信息；仅保存历史轨迹不足，记忆必须保留对未来动作有用的信息。核心疑问是：无记忆的基础模型能否仅靠动作示范学会保留并使用历史信息，而不依赖外部记忆模块或记忆专用标注。

Method: 在预训练VLA策略内部发展该能力：用测试时训练（test-time training）通过在线自监督更新把观测历史编码为紧凑的fast weights，避免反复处理完整历史；设计观测锚定（observation-grounded）接口，从视觉-语言信息中提取记忆形成所需内容并向动作专家提供检索上下文；用动作监督塑造记忆保留与使用的内容，并采用记忆-策略交替学习，使优化时各组件都有固定的对应方。

Result: 在16个RoboMME任务上，平均成功率由无记忆基策略的17.93%提升至56.83%，优于该基准中报告的循环记忆方法；受控性能剖析显示相对显式记忆方法至少有3倍推理加速。

Conclusion: 无需外部推理模型与记忆专用标注，预训练VLA可借助测试时快速权重获得可用的记忆能力，兼顾性能与推理效率，为记忆依赖操作提供了一条可扩展路径。

Abstract: Memory-dependent robotic manipulation requires policies to use information that is no longer available in the current observation. Retaining history alone is insufficient: memory must preserve information that supports future actions. One challenge is whether a memory-free foundation model can learn to retain and use historical information from action demonstrations alone, without external memory support. We introduce T$^2$Mem, a framework that develops this capability within a pretrained vision-language-action policy, without external reasoning models or memory-specific annotations. T$^2$Mem uses test-time training to encode observation history into compact fast weights through online self-supervised updates, avoiding repeated processing of the full history. An observation-grounded interface extracts vision-language information for memory formation and supplies retrieved context to the action expert. Action supervision shapes what the memory learns to retain and use, while alternating memory-policy learning gives each component a fixed counterpart during optimization. Across 16 RoboMME tasks, T$^2$Mem improves average success from 17.93% to 56.83% over the memory-free base policy and outperforms the recurrent-memory methods reported in the benchmark, while controlled profiling indicates at least 3x inference speedup over explicit methods. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [274] [Degeneracy-Orthogonal Geometric Constraints for LiDAR SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36753)
*Minseo Kim,Yina Kim,Jinhwa Hwang,Alex Junho Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出退化正交截面偏移描述子DeCOD，用截面几何地标约束长隧道/管道中LiDAR里程计的纵向漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴对齐均匀走廊（长隧道、管道）中，LiDAR里程计沿弱特征行进方向存在不可约束的漂移，这种结构性退化仅靠局部scan matching无法解决。

Method: 设计结构对齐的几何描述子DeCOD：对估计边界轮廓的有符号法向偏差进行参数化编码截面边界（管接缝、结构环）地标；匹配时显式消解航向歧义，并通过畸变估计解耦一阶轮廓误差；匹配地标生成几何因子，在位姿图优化中约束截面位置与走廊轴向对齐，同时不约束绕共同轴的旋转。

Result: 在公开基准与实地实验中，DeCOD的地标检索鲁棒性优于标准3D描述子，可跨不同里程计前端稳定轨迹，在几何退化下可靠约束纵向漂移。

Conclusion: 利用截面地标作为退化轴上的度量约束，是缓解轴向均匀环境中LiDAR SLAM纵向漂移的有效途径；方法可与多种里程计前端兼容。

Abstract: Autonomous robot navigation relies on simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) to estimate motion and maintain an accurate pose within an environment. However, in axially uniform corridors such as long tunnels and pipelines, LiDAR odometry is fundamentally limited by unconstrained drift along the feature-weak travel direction. This structural degeneracy cannot be resolved by local scan matching alone. To address this challenge, we propose the Degeneracy-orthogonal Contour Offset Descriptor (DeCOD), a structure-aligned geometric descriptor for cross-sectional landmarks. Cross-sectional boundaries, such as pipe joints and structural rings, provide metric constraints along this degenerate axis, but distinguishing individual landmarks requires capturing subtle surface variations across nearly identical profiles. The descriptor parameterizes signed normal deviation from estimated boundary contours, and matching explicitly resolves heading ambiguity and decouples first-order contour errors by distortion estimation. Matched landmarks yield geometric factors that enforce agreement in cross-section position and corridor axis alignment during pose-graph optimization, correcting longitudinal drift while leaving rotation about the common axis unconstrained. On a public benchmark and in field experiments, DeCOD achieves robust landmark retrieval over standard 3D descriptors and successfully stabilizes trajectories across different odometry frontends, reliably constraining longitudinal drift under geometric degeneracy.

</details>


### [275] [LexiconVLA: Learning Reusable Atomic Action Codebooks for Unseen Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36774)
*Zeming Wei,Jianheng Ye,Xinshuai Song,Sirui Chen,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LexiconVLA，用可检索的原子动作词典（全局+细节码本）跨任务复用交互结构，通过视觉-原子动作对齐与轨迹重建学习码本，无需技能专家或部署时更新，在 RLBench 未见任务上显著提升成功率并完成真机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型难以复用未见任务中反复出现的交互模式；诊断发现任务整体成功并不代表其组成原子动作在不同任务上下文中被一致执行，说明缺乏可迁移的原子动作抽象。

Method: 构建可检索的原子动作词典：全局码本捕获共享交互结构，细节码本刻画细粒度执行变化；用视觉-原子动作对齐将“由视觉状态变化重建轨迹”与“由动作码预测视觉结果”耦合，基于 AtomAction 数据集（69 任务、57,803 片段）联合轨迹重建与视觉对齐学习码本；再由规划器与场景感知适配器把新目标翻译为码条件子任务，交给共享策略执行。

Result: 在 26 个 RLBench 任务、5 种策略主干上，18 个已见任务性能基本保持，8 个策略训练未见任务成功率提升；与 BridgeVLA 结合后未见任务成功率从 16.67% 升至 34.17%（+17.50 个百分点），总体成功率 71.08%，为已报告结果中最高；真机实验展示了分步执行与失败恢复。

Conclusion: 可检索的原子动作词典为 VLA 提供无需专家策略与部署时参数更新的跨任务复用机制，兼顾已见任务稳定性与未见任务泛化，并具备真机可行性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models struggle to reuse recurring interactions in unseen tasks. Our diagnostic study reveals that reliable task completion does not imply consistent execution of constituent atomic actions across task contexts. We present LexiconVLA, a retrievable atomic-action lexicon for cross-task reuse. Global and detail codebooks capture shared interaction structure and fine-grained execution variation, respectively, preserving both reusable patterns and execution details. Visual-Atomic Action Alignment couples trajectory reconstruction from visual state changes with visual outcome prediction from action codes, grounding the lexicon in motion and its effects. We learn these codebooks with trajectory reconstruction and visual alignment on our AtomAction Dataset of 57,803 segments from 69 tasks. A planner and scene-aware adapter translate new goals into code-conditioned subtasks for a shared policy, without skill-specific experts or deployment-time parameter updates. Across five policy backbones on 26 RLBench tasks, LexiconVLA largely maintains performance on 18 seen tasks while improving success on 8 tasks held out from policy training. With BridgeVLA, unseen-task success rises from 16.67% to 34.17% (+17.50 percentage points), and overall success reaches 71.08%, the highest among methods with reported results. Real-robot experiments demonstrate stepwise execution and failure recovery.

</details>


### [276] [DRHeC: Differentiable Rendering for Hand-Eye Calibration with RGB-Based Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36779)
*Xiaotian Zhang,Yusheng Wang,Naoya Kagawa,Noritaka Takamura,Keiji Okuhara,Hiroyasu Baba,Jun Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于RGB的可微渲染手眼标定框架，引入颜色与掩码几何特征，并用掩码引导的图像翻译保证一致性；真实UR5e上抓取88.9%、插入57.4%，显著超越EasyHeC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标记手眼标定中，现有可微渲染方法（EasyHeC类）仅用二值掩码渲染对比，丢失内部轮廓细节导致精度受限，且优化不稳定、易陷入局部极小。

Method: 构建RGB可微渲染手眼标定框架：在渲染-观测比对中同时融入颜色与掩码几何特征以提供更丰富的几何与外观线索；并提出掩码引导的图像到图像翻译方法，在翻译过程中显式保持颜色与几何一致性。

Result: 仿真与真实实验均验证了精度与鲁棒性提升，优于现有可微渲染方法；UR5e真实实验中抓取成功率88.9%、插入成功率57.4%，分别比SOTA方法EasyHeC高出46.3和48.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 用RGB多线索可微渲染替代纯二值掩码，可同时提升手眼标定精度与优化稳定性；该标定使无标记机械臂在真实抓取与插入任务上取得大幅性能增益。

Abstract: Accurate hand-eye calibration is crucial for precision manipulation. Traditional methods rely on markers, with their precision dependent on marker accuracy and observability. In contrast, markerless methods, such as learning-based approaches, use deep neural networks to directly extract keypoints or features from images, enabling the computation of hand-eye transformation with a single image and without the need for physical markers. Recently, differentiable rendering-based methods for hand-eye calibration have leveraged physical models to render binary masks and compare them with observations, enabling hand-eye calibration without fiducial markers in the calibration stage and providing interpretable optimization. While the state-of-the-art differentiable rendering methods achieve remarkable accuracy, the use of binary masks can result in the loss of internal profile details, reducing precision. Additionally, these methods can also suffer from unstable optimization and local minima. In this study, we propose a novel RGB-based differentiable rendering framework that provides richer geometric and appearance cues by incorporating color and mask geometric features, thereby improving calibration accuracy and optimization stability. Additionally, we propose a mask-guided image-to-image translation method to ensure explicit preservation of color and geometric consistency throughout the translation. Our approach is validated through both simulation and real-world experiments, with results demonstrating strong accuracy and robustness and clear improvements over existing differentiable rendering methods. Our method achieves a grasping success rate of 88.9% and insertion success rate of 57.4% on the UR5e real-world experiment, outperforming the state-of-the-art differentiable rendering hand-eye calibration method EasyHeC by 46.3 and 48.1 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [277] [Scale-Invariant Manipulability Shape Tracking Across Heterogeneous Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36784)
*Geunwoo Kwon,Dong-gyu Lee,Kai Li,Soonwoong Hwang,Wansoo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出尺度不变的可操作性形状跟踪方法，用单位行列式矩阵代表消解绝对尺度差异，在AIRM度量下推导微分与正交坐标表示，并嵌入约束型关节速度QP中，实现异构机器人及人到机器人的可操作性形状迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨尺寸、跨运动学结构迁移可操作性时，目标往往只是复现椭球朝向与半轴比例，而非绝对尺度。全矩阵跟踪会同时惩罚形状与绝对尺度差异，在仅需形状匹配时产生不必要的代价与调参负担。

Method: 将仅差正标量因子的矩阵视为等价，取其单位行列式代表作为形状描述。在仿射不变黎曼度量（AIRM）下推导单位行列式形状代表的微分，以及切空间跟踪残差的正交坐标表示。所得尺度不变目标与位置、末端方向任务一起集成到带约束的关节速度二次规划中。

Result: 在四台异构机器人上做机器人间与人到机器人迁移。三台跟随者无需尺度调参即达9.30×10^-5的末端形状距离。带机器人专属目标尺度调参时，Full方法在KR500与UR20上末端轴比误差0.19–0.31。人臂伸展并发任务下，本方法在四台机器人上均产生沿X拉长的双力形状，与人类目标一致，末端位置误差为参考臂长的2.4–5.6%，而Full方法跟踪原始人类椭球时误差高达75%。

Conclusion: 形状（而非绝对尺度）的可操作性迁移可通过单位行列式代表与AIRM几何统一建模，并在QP框架内与位置、方向任务协同求解；仿真结果显示其免尺度调参、形状保持与人到机器人迁移方面显著优于全矩阵跟踪。

Abstract: When transferring manipulability across systems with different sizes and kinematic structures, matching absolute ellipsoid scale may be unnecessary when the goal is to reproduce orientation and semi-axis length ratios. Full-matrix tracking, however, penalizes both shape and absolute-scale differences, even when only shape matching is required. We therefore propose a scale-invariant manipulability shape-tracking method that treats matrices differing only by a positive scalar factor as equivalent and uses their unit-determinant representatives. We derive the differential of the unit-determinant shape representative and an orthonormal coordinate representation of the tangent tracking residual under the affine-invariant Riemannian metric (AIRM). The resulting scale-invariant objective is integrated with position and end-effector direction tasks in a constrained joint-velocity quadratic program. Simulations with four heterogeneous robots evaluate robot-to-robot and human-to-robot transfer. On three followers, the proposed method achieves endpoint shape distances of 9.30 x 10^-5 without scale tuning. With robot-specific target scales tuned during motion, the Full method retains endpoint axis-ratio errors of 0.19-0.31 on KR500 and UR20. For human reaching with concurrent tasks, the proposed method yields dual force shapes elongated along X like the human target on all four robots, with endpoint position errors of 2.4-5.6% of reference arm length versus up to 75% for the Full method tracking the original human ellipsoid.

</details>


### [278] [TaRL: Learning General and Physical Rewards from Tactile Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36785)
*Po-Yi Wu,Dao-Jan Chang,Shang-Ya Hsiao,Hong-Ming Chen,Yu-Cheng Su,Tsung-Wei Ke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TaRL 从触觉形变序列中回归任务完成进度，作为塑形奖励提升接触丰富操作任务的样本效率与成功率，并可跨物体泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作需要精确的接触序列、稳定的抓取与定向施力，强化学习虽可自动习得这些行为，但性能严重依赖奖励设计：稀疏奖励效率低，密集奖励难以人工指定。现有视觉奖励学习仅依赖视觉观测，无法捕捉视觉目标之外的奖励信号，尤其是抓取力与力方向等局部交互信息。

Method: 提出 Tactile Reward Learning（TaRL）：以触觉形变图（tactile deformation map）序列为输入，从成功与失败演示中回归任务完成进度，作为强化学习的塑形奖励。由于捕捉的是机器人-物体局部交互，可提供关于稳固抓取与正确施力方向的信息，且对物体位置等场景布局变化具有鲁棒性。在仿真四个任务与真实世界两个任务上评估，并与视觉奖励结合。

Result: 作为塑形奖励显著提升样本效率与最终成功率：仿真中螺母穿线成功率由 34% 提升至 56%，真实世界中立方体抓取由 37% 提升至 97%。触觉与视觉奖励结合可进一步提升性能；在盒子放置任务上训练后直接部署到罐子放置任务，仍显著改善新任务上的策略学习，显示出跨物体实例泛化能力。

Conclusion: 触觉演示可用于学习非视觉、聚焦局部交互的奖励信号，弥补视觉奖励学习的不足，并在仿真与真实机器人上带来一致提升；与视觉奖励互补且具备跨实例泛化潜力，为接触丰富操作提供了一条可行的奖励设计路径。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires robots to sequence precise contacts, maintain stable grasps, and apply directed forces. Reinforcement learning (RL) can acquire such behaviors automatically, but its performance hinges on reward design: sparse rewards reduce the learning efficiency, while dense rewards are hard to specify. Visual reward learning addresses this by inferring rewards from action-free demonstrations. Because it conditions only on visual observations, it fails to capture rewards beyond visual goals. We propose Tactile Reward Learning (TaRL), a framework that learns rewards from tactile demonstrations. TaRL takes a sequence of tactile deformation maps as input, and regresses task-completion progress from both successful and failed demonstrations. Because TaRL captures local robot-object interaction, it provides informative feedback to learn firm grasps and correctly directed forces; meanwhile, it is robust to changes in scene layout such as object position. We evaluate TaRL on four manipulation tasks in simulation and two in the real world. Used as a shaping reward, it substantially improves both sample efficiency and final success rate, raising success on Nut threading from 34% to 56% in simulation and on cube pickup from 37% to 97% in the real world. Combining tactile with visual rewards improves performance further. TaRL also generalizes across object instances: trained on box placement and directly deployed to can placement, it significantly improves policy learning on the new task. Project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [279] [Spotter: Let the Embodied Model Lead, and the VLM Reflect for It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36808)
*Long Li,Qichao Zhao,Yue Yang,Fan Xu,Zhe Wang,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Chao Qu,Heng Tao Shen,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Spotter：让具身模型主导连续执行，VLM 并行监控并在检测到错误时介入反思与纠正，从而赋予具身模型"自我纠错"能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身模型只在成功示范上训练，输入信息狭窄，因此在失败后几乎无法自我修复（既不能靠随机重试，也不能靠语言描述的纠错信息，best-of-N 也无法选出成功候选）。作者认为反思必须由信息量更大、更通用的 VLM 承担。

Method: 反转 VLM 主导范式：具身模型居于关键路径，连续执行任务并保持控制权；VLM 并行运行，通过轻量级本地 screener 监控轨迹，仅在确认错误时介入，反思并纠正错误后交还控制权。用 Qwen 与 GPT 两种 VLM 分别实例化 Spotter。

Result: GPT 版 Spotter 在 RoboCasa 上把 Cosmos Policy 与 π0.5 分别提升 5.6 和 7.5 个百分点；将 π0.5 在 RoboTwin 2.0 Hard 设置上从 47.2% 提到 57.0%，在真机上从 53% 提到 83%。Qwen 版成功回合仅比纯具身模型多耗时 13–16 秒，比同模型的 VLM 主导基线少约 70% 时间。

Conclusion: 具身智能体的失败修复需要外部通用模型提供的反思能力；以"具身模型主导、VLM 异步监控按需介入"的角色反转设计，能在低开销下显著提升多种具身策略的成功率，包括真机部署。

Abstract: Current embodied models do not respond to their own failures, although what just went wrong could inform a small adjustment on the next attempt, the kind of reflection behind the gains of thinking in language models. We test whether they can repair a known error, which requires producing a correction and judging whether it is right. Stopped at a failure and allowed to retry, they seldom repair it through their own randomness or from a language description of the error, and best-of-N selection cannot pick the successful candidate after a failure. We attribute this to training only on successful demonstrations and to inputs too narrow to show what went wrong, and conclude that reflection must come from a vision-language model (VLM), which takes in far more information, such as the episode history and text, and is more general. Prior VLM-led work has the VLM plan every step and invoke the embodied model as a tool, placing the VLM on the critical path. We propose Spotter, which reverses the roles: the embodied model leads and executes continuously, while the VLM runs in parallel, monitors through a lightweight local screener, intervenes only when an error is detected, reflects on and corrects it, and returns control. We run Spotter with Qwen and with GPT as the VLM, and both improve the embodied models; with GPT, Spotter improves Cosmos Policy and $\pi_{0.5}$ by 5.6 and 7.5 percentage points on RoboCasa, and raises $\pi_{0.5}$ from 47.2% to 57.0% on the Hard setting of RoboTwin 2.0 and from 53% to 83% on a real robot. Because the VLM steps in only when an error is confirmed, a successful episode with Qwen takes only 13 to 16 s longer than with the embodied model alone and about 70% less time than with a VLM-led baseline using the same model. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [280] [VidAct: Learning Manipulation from In-the-Wild Videos with Object-Centric 3D Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36870)
*Hang Li,Mingxin Zhang,Zihan Wu,Yang Tian,Dong Chen,Fengyi Shen,Yuan Meng,Xiangtong Yao,Heng Zhang,Ziyuan Liu,Zhenshan Bing,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VidAct：从单目演示视频重建物体网格与运动，在静态物体坐标系中规范化运动，并用残差轨迹迁移适配新物体配置；策略学习时以仿真特权完整物体点云为辅助监督，但部署仅用RGB，实现零样本真机操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人数据昂贵，视频演示是可扩展替代；但现有重建式方法受限于固定视角或人-机动作重定向，轨迹难以在不形变的前提下适配新物体布局，且策略缺乏物体级3D几何感知，影响物体定位与形状理解，制约精细操作。

Method: 三部分：1）从任意演示视频重建物体网格与运动，并在静态物体坐标系中规范化，免除具身特定重定向、兼容多视角；2）残差轨迹迁移，将重建运动适配到新物体配置同时保持运动形状；3）策略学习中以仿真提供的特权完整物体点云逐帧预测作为辅助任务，部署时仅用RGB，提供对物体位姿与3D几何的密集物体中心监督。

Result: 在人类、机器人、生成及互联网视频上验证了广泛的视频适用性与零样本部署；逐帧完整物体3D监督提升策略泛化与仿真到真实成功率；残差轨迹迁移实现可靠的轨迹适配并更好保持形状。

Conclusion: VidAct以物体中心、3D感知的方式打通单目视频到机器人策略的路径，兼顾视角鲁棒性与轨迹可迁移性，为低成本视频驱动操作学习提供可行方案。

Abstract: Video demonstrations offer a scalable alternative to costly robot data for learning manipulation, yet existing reconstruction-based approaches often rely on constrained camera viewpoints or human-to-robot retargeting, while the reconstructed trajectories are difficult to adapt to new objects configurations without distorting the trajectory shape. Another key limitation is that the resulting policies often lack precise object-level 3D geometry awareness, limiting object grounding and object shape awareness critical for precise manipulation. To bridge these gaps, we propose VidAct, an efficient video-to-robot framework that learns object-centric, 3D-aware manipulation policies from a single monocular video per task and enables zero-shot real-world deployment. VidAct consists of three key components. First, VidAct reconstructs object meshes and motion from arbitrary demo videos and canonicalizes the motion in the static object frame, avoiding embodiment-specific retargeting and accommodating diverse camera viewpoints. Second, VidAct employ residual trajectory transfer for adapting the reconstructed motion to novel object configurations while preserving its motion shape. Finally, as the key policy-learning component, VidAct predicts simulation-provided privileged complete-object point clouds at each frame as an auxiliary task while retaining RGB-only deployment, providing dense object-centric supervision over both object pose and 3D geometry. Experiments on human, robot, generated, and internet videos demonstrate broad video applicability and zero-shot deployment. Per-frame complete-object 3D supervision improves policy generalization and sim-to-real success, while residual trajectory transfer enables reliable trajectory adaptation with better shape preservation.

</details>


### [281] [PreferenceFlow: Test-Time Guidance of Flow-Matching Robot Policies from Human Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36872)
*Yiqi Tang,Diyuan Shi,Runze Li,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PreferenceFlow：用人类干预片段构建偏好模型，在测试时以QGF采样+偏好梯度引导冻结的流匹配策略，无需环境奖励，在Franka四项精密插入任务上把成功率从69%提升到90.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配策略能表达复杂机器人行为，但部署时在分布偏移下易出现局部误差；而常规RL策略改进依赖难以在真实操作中指定或获取的奖励信号，因此需要一种无需环境奖励、不更新基础策略的测试时策略改进方法。

Method: 从同一初始条件状态出发，配对"人类干预动作块"与"机器人生成动作块"以训练偏好模型；推理时采用QGF采样更新，将其中的价值梯度替换为在估计的干净动作处评估的偏好梯度；并引入梯度上限损失（惩罚干预对上的过大梯度）与零梯度损失（抑制在专家示范动作附近的引导）。

Result: 在Franka机器人四项真实精密插入任务上，平均成功率90.5%，相比冻结基线策略69%显著提升；消融实验支持梯度正则化与正确排序的偏好标签在评测设置中的作用。

Conclusion: 人类干预可作为局部偏好监督，在基础策略冻结、无环境奖励的条件下有效引导生成式机器人策略，验证了测试时偏好引导的实用性。

Abstract: Flow-matching policies can represent complex robot behaviors but remain susceptible to local errors under distribution shift at deployment. Many reinforcement learning approaches to policy improvement require reward signals that are difficult to specify or obtain in real-world manipulation. We present PreferenceFlow, a framework for improving a pretrained flow policy at test time without environment rewards or updates to the base policy. Human intervention chunks are paired with robot chunks generated from the same initial conditioning state to train a preference model. During infer- ence, we adopt the QGF sampling update, replacing its value gradient with the preference gradient evaluated at an estimated clean action. A gradient-cap loss penalizes excessive gradients on intervention pairs, while a zero-gradient loss discourages guidance near actions from expert demonstrations. On four real-world precision insertion tasks with a Franka robot, Pref- erenceFlow achieves a mean success rate of 90.5%, compared with 69% for the frozen policy. Ablations support the roles of gradient regularization and correctly ordered preference labels in the evaluated settings. These results demonstrate the utility of human interventions as local preference supervision for guiding frozen generative robot policies.

</details>


### [282] [NIDAR: NIR-Guided Intrinsic Decomposition for Scalable Scene-Agnostic LiDAR Intensity Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36878)
*Junjie Zhang,Jie Yin,Kefei Qian,Jie Li,Mengpei Jia,Yajuan Dun,Wenbo Chu,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NIDAR：基于RGB外观与仿真几何的前馈式LiDAR强度合成框架，无需目标场景强度标注或逐场景优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR仿真多缺失返回强度，或依赖需真实强度监督与逐场景优化的重建方法，导致采集与拟合成本高、跨仿真场景复用性差。

Method: 融合预训练伪近红外（pseudo-NIR）翻译、分层本征分解、几何感知调制与源域分布校准；全部可学习组件用Waymo离线训练，评估时权重与校准固定，直接迁移到Waymo与nuScenes仿真点云。

Result: 逐像素精度与基线相当，结构保真度与感知保真度更优；消融诊断显示伪NIR先验经"反射率—重映射"路径优于直接回归；已集成至UE5、Isaac Sim与生成式LiDAR管线，两个仿真室内场景中强度感知SLAM显示潜在下游价值。

Conclusion: NIDAR提供可扩展的强度合成接口，但跨波长、相机配置、嵌入式部署与真实传感器验证仍待完成。

Abstract: LiDAR return intensity provides complementary surface-response cues for robotic perception and state estimation, yet many simulation pipelines omit it or reproduce it using reconstruction methods that require real intensity supervision and per-scene optimization. These requirements increase data-collection and fitting costs and limit reuse across simulated scenes. We present NIDAR, a feed-forward framework that synthesizes dense intensity-like observations from RGB appearance and simulator geometry. NIDAR combines pretrained pseudo-NIR translation, hierarchical intrinsic decomposition, geometry-aware modulation, and source-domain distribution calibration to transfer reflectance-related image cues to simulated point clouds. Its learned components are trained offline using Waymo data; their weights and calibration remain fixed during evaluation on Waymo and nuScenes. Deployment therefore requires neither target-scene intensity labels nor target-scene gradient-based fitting. The reported comparisons show competitive pixel-wise accuracy and favorable structural and perceptual fidelity against the evaluated reconstruction baselines. A controlled pseudo-NIR-versus-RGB diagnostic further shows that the pseudo-NIR prior is most beneficial when used through the paper-aligned reflectance-and-remapping route, rather than as a simple direct intensity regressor. We further integrate NIDAR with Unreal Engine 5, Isaac Sim, and a generative LiDAR pipeline. Two intensity-aware SLAM systems evaluated in two simulated indoor scenes suggest potential downstream utility, but do not constitute real-robot validation. NIDAR therefore offers a scalable intensity-synthesis interface for the evaluated settings; cross-wavelength, camera-configuration, embedded, and real-sensor validation remain future work.

</details>


### [283] [All Roads Lead to Rome: Flow-driven Multi-Anchor Exploration for Open-Environment Active 3D Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36889)
*Yang Li,Aming Wu,Zihao Zhang,Ziju Han,Sijia Zhang,Yahong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将开放环境主动3D建图中的长时程目标预测重新表述为基于条件流匹配的条件多模态锚点生成，替代传统单点确定性预测，并通过障碍感知规划、探索模式聚类与分层选择生成最终可执行轨迹，提升泛化性与重建效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动建图方法在长时程探索中通常预测单一粗粒度远距离目标再转化为路径，属于单点确定性预测。在部分可观测条件下，同一局部观测可能对应多种合理的探索方向，确定性预测易产生脆弱决策，导致在未见环境中泛化性差，作者实验也验证这是泛化弱的关键因素。

Method: 将长时程目标预测建模为条件多模态锚点生成：用条件流匹配（Conditional Flow Matching）学习当前建图状态到粗粒度探索锚点的条件分布。锚点经障碍感知规划转为可执行候选路径；再用探索模式聚类压缩几何相似轨迹、降低候选冗余；最后由分层选择模块选出最优模式并在模式内重排路径，输出最终可执行轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在开放环境中相比基线提升了泛化能力与重建效率；并验证单点确定性预测是现有方法泛化弱的关键因素之一。

Conclusion: 以条件多模态生成替代单点预测可缓解部分可观测下的决策脆弱性，是开放环境主动3D建图长时程探索的有效范式，配合聚类与分层选择可兼顾多样性与可执行性。

Abstract: To advance the development of embodied intelligence, Open-Environment Active 3D Mapping has attracted increasing attention, aiming to perform a long-horizon and shortest trajectory exploration for reconstructing unseen scenarios. Since only limited information about unseen environments is available, methods built on the closed-set assumption, i.e., assuming that the test environments are similar to those seen during training, cannot generalize satisfactorily. In existing active mapping methods, long-horizon exploration is often guided by predicting a coarse long-range goal and then converting it into an executable path. However, this stage is usually formulated as single-point prediction. Under partial observability, the same local observation may correspond to multiple plausible exploration directions, making such deterministic prediction prone to brittle decisions and degraded performance in unseen scenarios. Our experiments further verify that this is a key factor underlying their weak generalization. To address this issue, we reformulate long-horizon target prediction as conditional multimodal anchor generation using Conditional Flow this http URL of predicting a single goal, our method learns a conditional distribution over coarse exploration anchors from the current mapping state. These anchors are first converted into executable candidate paths through obstacle-aware planning. We then apply exploration-mode clustering to compress geometrically similar trajectories and reduce candidate redundancy. Finally, a hierarchical selection module selects the most promising mode and reranks paths within it to produce the final executable trajectory. Experiments show that our method improves generalization and reconstruction efficiency in open environments.

</details>


### [284] [AeroManip-VLA: Scalable Vision-Language-Action Learning for Aerial Manipulation with RL-Generated Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36915)
*Rui Huang,Yanlin Mu,Lidong Li,Yucong Wang,Zichen Yan,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AeroManip-VLA：面向空中机械臂的可扩展仿真基准，用RL策略+专家规则自动生成VLA演示数据，并支持细粒度轨迹分析与策略评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机械臂把操作拓展到地面机器人难以到达的三维空间，但将VLA迁移到空中平台面临操作与飞行强耦合、观测持续变化、安全关键等难题，需要多样数据与系统评估；而真机采集与评估成本高、难扩展、难复现。

Method: 构建GPU加速、大规模并行的仿真框架，包含低层载荷感知的飞行与操作控制；把可复用强化学习策略与专家任务规则结合，在多样物体、环境与随机初始条件下自动生成无需遥操作的演示（含抓取、放置等基本技能与需导航+操作的长程任务）；引入自动事件标注与轨迹分类，用于过滤演示并分析任务进度、行为结果与安全失效；在此基础上评测多种模仿学习与VLA基线。

Result: 在多种任务设置下评测了多种模仿学习与VLA基线，揭示了其性能特征与失败模式，并实现无需人工遥操作的可扩展演示生成与结构化轨迹分析。

Conclusion: AeroManip-VLA为空中操作提供了仿真中的数据生成、轨迹分析与VLA系统评估能力，可在真机部署前进行低成本、可复现的规模化验证。

Abstract: Aerial manipulators extend robotic manipulation into 3D workspaces that are difficult for ground-based robots to access, creating new opportunities for general-purpose manipulation. However, extending Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models to aerial robots introduces distinct challenges due to the tight coupling between manipulation and flight, continuously changing observations, and safety-critical physical interactions. These challenges demand diverse training data and systematic policy evaluation, yet collecting demonstrations and evaluating policies directly on physical aerial platforms are costly, difficult to scale, and hard to repeat under controlled conditions. We present AeroManip-VLA, a scalable benchmark for aerial VLA data generation and policy evaluation. AeroManip-VLA provides a GPU-accelerated simulation framework with low-level payload-aware flight and manipulation control in massively parallel environments. Building on this framework, we combine reusable reinforcement learning policies with expert task rules to automatically generate demonstrations without human teleoperation across diverse objects, environments, and randomized initial conditions. The generated data include basic skills such as grasping and placing, as well as long-horizon tasks that require both navigation and manipulation. We further introduce automated event labeling and trajectory categorization to filter demonstrations. These mechanisms enable fine-grained analysis of task progress, behavioral outcomes, and safety-related failures. Finally, we evaluate a range of imitation learning and VLA baselines across different task settings, revealing their performance characteristics and failure modes. Together, AeroManip-VLA enables scalable aerial manipulation data generation, structured trajectory analysis, and systematic VLA evaluation in simulation prior to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [285] [Track-and-Complete: Learning Humanoid Skills from a Single Failed Human Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36924)
*Sarmad Idrees,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TRACC流水线，从失败的人体演示视频中学习人形机器人技能：先模仿失败前的有效运动前缀，再依据推断的任务目标用任务完成奖励引导策略完成剩余任务，无需成功示范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形技能视频学习通常依赖成功的人体示范，数据采集成本高。失败视频中仍包含可用信息：失败前的有效轨迹前缀，以及所意图达成的任务结果，这些信息此前仅被当作负例使用，未被充分利用。

Method: TRACC两阶段流水线：1）识别并模仿失败前可用的运动轨迹前缀，作为先验知识；2）从视频推断任务意图结果，在失败点之后用任务完成奖励（而非成功轨迹监督）引导策略学习完成任务目标。

Result: 在Oops!数据集的六个in-the-wild失败人体任务上评估，结果证明在无成功示范条件下从失败尝试中学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 失败人体视频可作为人形技能学习的可行监督来源，扩展了模仿学习的可用数据范围。

Abstract: Learning humanoid skills from videos typically requires a successful human demonstration, which often demands custom data collection. Although failures have traditionally been treated only as negative examples in robot learning, they can still reveal a usable trajectory prefix before the task fails, as well as the intended outcome. To leverage this information from a failed-attempt video, we propose TRACC, a pipeline that imitates the useful portion of the motion trajectory and then completes the task based on the inferred task outcome. The usable motion prefix serves as prior knowledge until the failure occurs, after which the task-completion reward guides the policy to learn the intended task goal without requiring a successful task trajectory. We evaluate our method on six in-the-wild failed human tasks from the Oops! dataset. Our experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach for learning from failed attempts when no successful demonstration is available. Thus, these findings establish failed human videos as a viable source of supervision for humanoid skill learning.

</details>


### [286] [ComManip: Overfitting Manipulation Policies to Comfortable Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36928)
*Yidan Lai,Xinyi Chen,Shuquan Man,Huiping Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ComManip学习范式：发现操作策略在紧凑、视觉与运动学稳定的"舒适操作区域"内训练成功率更高，通过推理时移动基座使目标进入该区域，在有限示教预算下将多种策略架构的任务成功率提升约20个百分点以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人示教数据采集成本高，示教预算有限。现有方法为提升泛化性，在采集时对物体位置、视角、机器人构型施加大量扰动，但有限预算下迫使策略拟合过度多样的视觉观测，局部条件下的观测-动作对应监督不足，反而降低成功率。

Method: 先验证"舒适操作区域"现象：在紧凑、视觉与运动学稳定的区域内训练的相同预算策略成功率更高。基于此提出ComManip：仅在舒适区域内演示训练专门化策略；推理时检测目标中心，驱动移动基座重定位，直到目标中心落入由舒适区演示估计出的图像空间熟悉范围，再执行同一操作策略，从而覆盖多样目标位置。

Result: 在多任务、多示教预算、多策略家族（ACT、π0.5、RDT、OpenVLA-OFT、SmolVLA）上实验，大工作空间、有限示教预算下，不同架构策略任务成功率提升约20个百分点及以上。

Conclusion: 将操作策略专门化到舒适操作区域是一种更具数据高效性的学习范式；相比在有限预算下追求视觉观测多样性，围绕稳定区域专门化并以基座重定位补偿目标分布，更能提升移动操作性能。

Abstract: Training robot manipulation policies relies on costly robot demonstrations, making large-scale data collection impractical. Meanwhile, to improve policy generalization, existing approaches seek greater diversity in visual observations by varying object placements, viewpoints, and robot configurations during data collection. However, under a limited demonstration budget, this strategy forces the policy to model diverse visual observations, providing insufficient supervision to learn reliable observation-action correspondences under similar local conditions. Our study reveals that policies trained under this strategy achieve lower task success rates than those trained within a compact, visually and kinematically stable region. We refer to these stable regions as comfortable manipulation regions. To exploit this finding, we propose ComManip, a learning paradigm that specializes manipulation policies to comfortable manipulation regions. During inference, ComManip repositions the mobile base until the detected target center enters the familiar image-space range estimated from comfortable-region demonstrations. It then executes the same manipulation policy, enabling effective manipulation across diverse target locations. We conduct extensive experiments across multiple manipulation tasks, demonstration budgets, and policy families including ACT, $\pi_{0.5}$, RDT, OpenVLA-OFT, and SmolVLA. The results demonstrate that ComManip improves task success by roughly 20 percentage points or more across different policy architectures in large workspaces under limited demonstration budgets, suggesting that specializing manipulation policies to comfortable regions provides a more data-efficient learning paradigm for manipulation.

</details>


### [287] [Beyond Token Importance: Preserving Spatial Scaffolds for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36967)
*Jiayu Chen,Shuyong Gao,Jingkai Jia,Xiaosheng Bu,Jiyuan Fu,Lingyi Hong,Kaixun Jiang,Yipan Xu,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 构建Stride几何剪枝基线并引入空间覆盖半径指标诊断VLA视觉token剪枝，进而提出免训练方法GeoScaffold，仅保留20%视觉token仍保持93.2%成功率并获1.78倍prefill加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA视觉token剪枝多按任务级语义相关性挑选单个token，忽略机器人操作所需的空间信息；作者希望弄清为何纯几何采样的Stride基线在某些比例下反而优于语义与随机剪枝。

Method: 先用均匀采样一维展平视觉序列的Stride基线做对照，提出"空间覆盖半径"（剪枝后保留token集诱导的最大空间盲区）来刻画失败模式；据此提出免训练的GeoScaffold：将图像划分为空间区域，按任务相关性权重分配区域间token预算，区域内部用最远点采样选择"支架"token以降低局部覆盖半径。

Result: 在π0.5与LIBERO上，GeoScaffold仅保留20%视觉token即可保持平均93.2%成功率，相对未剪枝基线取得1.78倍prefill加速；分析显示保留token的空间结构与任务成功率强相关。

Conclusion: 可靠VLA剪枝不仅要保留任务相关token，还需保留场景的空间支架；免训练的空间覆盖感知剪枝可在大幅压缩视觉token的同时维持操作性能。

Abstract: Existing VLA pruning strategies primarily select individual visual tokens according to task-level semantic relevance, while overlooking the spatial information required for robotic manipulation. To examine this limitation, we construct a simple Stride baseline that uniformly samples tokens along the flattened one-dimensional visual sequence, representing a purely geometric pruning strategy. Surprisingly, Stride outperforms semantic pruning and random pruning at certain pruning ratios, but collapses when the token budget is only slightly reduced. We characterize this phenomenon through the spatial coverage radius, defined as the largest spatial blind spot induced by the retained token set after pruning. Our analysis reveals a strong correlation between the spatial structure of retained tokens and task success, suggesting that reliable VLA pruning requires preserving not only task-relevant tokens but also the spatial scaffold of the scene. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose GeoScaffold, a training-free visual token pruning method that partitions each image into spatial regions, allocates inter-region token budgets using task-relevance weights, and selects intra-region scaffold tokens via farthest point sampling to reduce the local coverage radius. On pi 0.5 and LIBERO, GeoScaffold retains only 20% of visual tokens while preserving a 93.2% average success rate, and achieves a 1.78 times prefill speedup over the unpruned baseline.

</details>


### [288] [All You Need Is Low Fidelity: Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real of Learned Robotic Fish Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36993)
*Liam Maloney,Simon Ramchandani,Mike Y. Michelis,Ronan Hinchet,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用低保真度、无尾迹无附加质量历史的准稳态流体模型训练无状态闭环控制器，通过限带节律轨迹生成器约束策略，实现零调参迁移到软体单电机机器鱼，在户外泳池完成目标到达、扰动抑制与分布外目标跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体欠驱动机器鱼控制器能力不足，而高保真仿真代价高昂；作者想验证是否可用极低保真仿真（牺牲尾迹与附加质量历史）学到可迁移的通用闭环策略，从而把物理约束从仿真器转移到控制器中。

Method: 平台为单电机腱驱动的软体机器鱼，策略仅使用硬件可测观测量。分阶段流水线做两项独立辨识：固定尾部动力学、拟合无状态准稳态流体模型；策略不直接控制尾部，而是输出给限带节律轨迹生成器。关键约束是生成器不能超出流体模型被辨识的频带范围。

Result: 策略不经任何微调直接部署于户外泳池，单一策略完成闭环目标到达、扰动抑制，以及分布外目标捕获与跟踪；迁移成功主要依赖频带约束而非仿真保真度。

Conclusion: 低保真仿真加结构化控制器约束足以实现零调参sim-to-real迁移，提示应将更多物理知识嵌入控制器而非仿真器，并引发"物理该放在哪一侧"的开放问题。

Abstract: Complex tasks for underwater robots remain limited by the capabilities of their controllers. Learning a better one for a soft, underactuated robotic fish trades simulator cost against fidelity. We show that an intentionally low-fidelity simulator is enough: a stateless, quasi-steady fluid model with no wake and no added-mass history suffices to learn a \emph{general}, closed-loop controller that transfers to hardware without tuning. Our platform is a soft, single-motor, tendon-driven fish whose policy observes only what the hardware can measure. A staged pipeline grounds the simulator in two independent identifications, fixing the tail dynamics and a stateless fluid model; the policy then acts through a band-limited rhythmic trajectory generator rather than commanding the tail directly. Deployed unchanged in an outdoor pool, a single policy performs closed-loop target reaching, disturbance rejection, and out-of-distribution target acquisition and tracking. The transfer rests on the constraint rather than the fidelity: the generator cannot leave the band over which the fluid was identified. This raises the question of how much of the physics can reside in the controller rather than in the simulator.

</details>


### [289] [FACT: Fidelity-Aware Construction of Articulated Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37067)
*Kuixiang Shao,Chuansen Nie,Yinuo Bai,Jiayuan Gu,Jingyi Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FACT: 一个 agentic（证据—诊断—修订）框架，逐步构建"铰接孪生体"，同时提升几何、接触与动力学三类保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉上合理的铰接资产在接触交互中仍可能失败或运动不准确，根因在于几何、碰撞代理与动力学响应三方面保真度不足，且现有流程缺乏可量化反馈驱动的迭代修正机制。

Method: 智能体在共享可编辑表示上驱动"证据采集—诊断—修订"闭环，自主选择测量与模型编辑，数值工具执行并验证更新。三步：(1) 由图像经特征规划、定向测量与诊断式细化重建可编辑铰接几何；(2) 以任务感知的局部分割修复碰撞代理，再做保真度约束压缩；(3) 从被动响应视频构建响应模型，用仿真残差指导模型修订并进行约束物理参数拟合。

Result: 几何重建优于基线；以更简单的碰撞代理实现更可靠的交互；相比直接参数推断，能更好复现留出（held-out）物理响应。

Conclusion: 以量化反馈驱动的 agentic 闭环，可系统性提升铰接物体的几何/接触/动力学保真度，为可交互仿真资产构建提供可复用的方法范式。

Abstract: Visually plausible articulated assets may still fail during contact interactions or exhibit inaccurate motion. We present FACT (Fidelity-Aware Construction of Articulated Twins), an agentic framework that progressively constructs articulated twins to improve geometry, contact, and dynamic fidelity. The agent drives an evidence--diagnosis--revision loop on a shared editable representation, selecting measurements and model edits using quantitative feedback, while numerical tools execute and validate the updates. It reconstructs editable articulated geometry from images through feature planning, targeted measurements, and diagnostic refinement. On this reference, it repairs collision proxies through task-aware local repartitioning before fidelity-constrained compression. Finally, it constructs response models from passive-response videos, using simulation residuals to guide model revision and constrained physical parameter fitting. Experiments show that FACT improves geometric reconstruction over baselines, enables more reliable interaction with simpler collision proxies, and better reproduces held-out physical responses than direct parameter inference.

</details>


### [290] [Predictive Safety Curricula for Robust Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37070)
*Ivan Ovinnikov,Pascal Sutter,Christian Gehring,Jordis Herrmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PSC 用分布化安全评论家预测未来安全代价，据此优先采样危险地形与前遇到的事件来重分配训练分布，不改任务奖励与优化损失；在 ANYmal-D 硬件上小腿碰撞降低 63%（三个随机种子全部改善），并在量产爬楼梯平台上消除观测到的碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人即使平均任务表现很高，罕见但后果严重的失败仍会残留；原因之一是标准课程主要调整任务难度，而非安全关键经验的分布。

Method: 训练一个分布化安全评论家（distributional safety critic），从策略 rollout 中学习未来安全代价的预测；用该预测同时对地形上下文和已遇到的随机事件进行优先级排序，形成课程分配，仅改变训练分布，任务奖励与策略优化损失保持不变。

Result: 在受控崎岖地形与量产系统上评估。相比标准地形递进、优势回放、学习进度课程，PSC 提升可靠性，在困难地形与观测退化下收益最大；原理可迁移到两套量产栈：ANYmal-D 硬件三个匹配种子下小腿碰撞率较学习进度课程降低 63%，且每个种子均下降；量产爬楼梯平台评测中消除观测到的小腿碰撞。

Conclusion: 学习到的未来安全代价预测可为训练经验分配提供有效信号，把训练导向罕见失效模式，从而提升运动可靠性并迁移到真实部署系统。

Abstract: Rare but consequential failures can persist in learned locomotion policies for legged robots even when average task performance is high, in part because standard curricula primarily adapt task difficulty rather than the distribution of safety-critical experience. We introduce Predictive Safety Curricula (PSC), a framework for allocating locomotion training experience using learned predictions of future safety cost. PSC trains a distributional safety critic from policy rollouts and uses its predictions to prioritize both terrain contexts and previously encountered randomized events. The resulting curriculum modifies the training distribution while leaving the task reward and policy-optimization loss unchanged. We evaluate PSC in controlled rough-terrain locomotion and in production locomotion systems. PSC improves reliability relative to standard terrain progression, advantage-based replay, and learning-progress curricula, with the largest gains on difficult terrain and under degraded observations. The same allocation principle transfers to two production locomotion stacks. On ANYmal-D hardware, PSC reduces shank-collision incidence by $63\%$ relative to the learning-progress curriculum across three matched training seeds, with a reduction in every seed. On a production stair-climbing platform, PSC eliminates observed shank collisions in the evaluated hardware trials. These results show that learned predictions of future safety cost can provide an effective signal for allocating training experience toward rare failure modes and improving locomotion reliability.

</details>


### [291] [From Sky to Soil: A Morphing Aerial-Ground Robot for Seed Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37072)
*Namai Chandra,Lining Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种几何辅助的三功能变形机器人，实现空中飞行、地面行驶与定深播种的“飞行-驱动-播种”一体化架构，硬件原型验证了地面重定位与种子埋入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空撒播可到达偏远恢复场地，但难以控制种子在土壤中的落位，缺乏深度控制与地面重复作业能力，因此需要一种兼具空中可达性与地面精准播种能力的平台。

Method: 采用几何辅助的变形机构：着陆后重构为四轮播种构型，电子耦合双电机驱动后臂外折形成地面车轮；前部托盘下压使推进电机与钻头齿轮系啮合。通过复用推进电机驱动钻土，省去专用钻头驱动器。播种流程为成孔—投种—地面重定位或恢复飞行构型。配套控制器发送重构、托盘与种子门指令，飞行控制由专用自动驾驶仪负责。

Result: 硬件原型验证了变形机构与播种机构，展示了地面重定位和种子埋入两项功能。

Conclusion: 该概念验证为通过协同变形与执行器复用实现空中-地面种子埋入提供了硬件基础，可作为后续系统化研究与田间验证的起点。

Abstract: Aerial seed broadcasting can reach remote restoration sites, but provides limited control over seed placement within the soil. This paper presents a geometry-assisted, tri-functional morphing robot that combines aerial access, ground locomotion, and controlled-depth seed embedding in a fly-drive-plant architecture. After landing, the platform reconfigures into a four-wheeled planting configuration: an electronically coupled dual-motor drive folds the rear arms outward to form ground wheels, while a descending front tray engages the propulsion motors with a drill gear train. Reusing the propulsion motors for drilling eliminates a dedicated drill drive. The planting sequence forms a hole, dispenses a seed, and allows the vehicle to reposition on the ground or return to its flight configuration. Ground mobility supports repeated planting without requiring a separate flight between adjacent sites. A companion controller issues reconfiguration, tray, and seed-gate commands, while a dedicated autopilot handles flight control. The morphing and planting mechanisms are validated using a hardware prototype that demonstrates ground repositioning and seed embedding. This proof of concept establishes a hardware basis for aerial-ground seed embedding through coordinated reconfiguration and actuator reuse.

</details>


### [292] [BCNav: Bearing-Conditioned Depth Policies for Sound Source Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37084)
*Yaozhong Kang,Jiang Wang,Takeshi Ashizawa,Benjamin Yen,Kazuhiro Nakadai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BCNav 提出解耦式音视觉导航框架：用 DOA 估计器输出声源方位角，导航策略仅处理深度图与方位角，通过注入标定噪声的模仿学习训练输出连续速度指令，实现真实机器人零声学微调的未知环境声源导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声源导航可让机器人超越视觉视野响应听觉事件。但现有方法依赖声学仿真器做联合音视觉学习，存在低保真、高成本导致的域差，且网格仿真继承的离散动作空间在物理机器人上带来运动学落差，难以可靠部署。

Method: 将声学模块与学习导航策略解耦：DOA 估计给出标量方位角，策略只输入深度图与方位角（两类输入的域差已被较好刻画）。采集最短路演示并注入标定后的方位角噪声，以模仿学习训练策略，直接输出可在轮式地面机器人执行的连续速度指令。

Result: 在仿真与真实机器人上验证：无需声学微调、无需先验建图、无需采集真实音频数据，即可在未知环境中完成声源导航。

Conclusion: 解耦声学与导航策略、以 DOA 作为中间接口，并以带噪声的连续动作模仿学习替代联合音视觉仿真训练，是缓解声学仿真域差与离散动作运动学落差的有效路径，具备真实世界可部署性。

Abstract: The ability to navigate toward sound sources extends a robot's reach beyond its visual field, enabling response to auditory events in unknown environments. To equip robots with this capability, existing methods couple acoustic and visual information through joint audio-visual learning in acoustic simulators. However, acoustic simulation is both low-fidelity and expensive, producing a domain gap that prevents reliable real-world deployment, while the discrete action spaces inherited from grid-based simulators introduce an additional kinematic gap on physical robots. To alleviate these issues, we propose BCNav, a decoupled framework that separates the acoustic module from the learned navigation policy using direction-of-arrival (DOA) estimation: an estimator provides a scalar bearing to the sound source, so the navigation policy only processes depth images and a bearing angle, two inputs whose domain gaps are well characterized. We collect shortest-path demonstrations with calibrated bearing noise injection and train the policy via imitation learning to output continuous velocity commands directly executable on ground robots. We demonstrate the method in simulation and on a physical robot, navigating unknown environments without any acoustic fine-tuning, prior mapping, or real-world audio data collection. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [293] [Multifunctional Locomotion Control of Multi-Jointed BURs with Swimming and gait Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37086)
*Takumi Asada,Hideo Furuhashi,Kenta Tabata,Renato Miyagusuku,Koichi Ozaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态仿生水下机器人（BUR）控制机制：用四条四轴腿鳍的同一多关节结构表达多种运动模式，基于势函数与传感器模态实现非线性行为切换，摆脱预定义规则式行为选择，并通过实物与仿真验证了2-3种行为间的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态仿生水下机器人需兼具游动与足式行走能力，但现有运动控制依赖规则式行为选择与设计者先验判断，行为库被限定在预设范围内，难以获得新的行为技能，泛化与自主性受限。

Method: 设计配备四条腿鳍、每条四轴的多关节机构；构建基于传感器模态的非线性控制器，利用势函数（potential function）实现多模态运动控制，不依赖预定义的条件-行为规则；通过实物平台与仿真，基于多类传感器数据与行为模式进行验证。

Result: 实物与仿真验证表明，引入势函数的控制方式可实现两种及三种行为之间的平滑转换，同一机构可表达多种运动模式。

Conclusion: 将控制方法实现为多模态控制器，有望提升水下机器人在探索任务中的适应性与应用范围；后续需进一步验证更复杂行为与真实水下环境下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal biomimetic underwater robots (BURs) can conduct underwater tasks suitable for the environment. Combining the characteristics of aquatic organisms enables the swimming and leggedlocomotion required for underwater exploration. Locomotion control mechanism relies on rule-based behavior selection and the designer's discretion. This limits the robot's ability to acquire new behavioral capabilities to the predetermined range of behaviors. To address these challenges, we propose a mechanism and control system that enables the expression of multimodal locomotion capabilities from the same multi-jointed structure. A mechanism equipped with four leg-fins each having four axes is used. This controller achieves nonlinear behavior based on sensor modalities, rather than relying on predefined conditional rule-based on locomotion functions. This system was validated through both physical and simulation testing based on multiple sensor data and behavioral patterns. Utilizing a potential function in multimodal locomotion control was verified to enable transitions between two or three behaviors. Implementing the control method as a multimodal controller is expected to enhance its application in underwater exploration. Our project page is at this https URL .

</details>


### [294] [V2X-WAM: A Cooperative World Action Model for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37098)
*Junwei You,Weizhe Tang,Can Wang,Yan Zhao,Jun Hua,Haotian Shi,Wei Zhang,Lin Wang,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: V2X-WAM：车路协同世界-动作模型，将协同场景理解、动作生成与未来世界推演紧耦合，用可靠性感知时空表征与量化通信消息，让规划轨迹显式条件化未来占据与动态流预测并反馈精修轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同驾驶方法多利用路侧信息增强当前场景表征，却很少显式建模未来驾驶动作的后果，导致规划器难以预判决策与交通环境演化之间的相互作用。作者希望把协同感知与未来世界推演结合，形成动作—未来世界闭环。

Method: 构建 V2X-WAM 协同世界-动作模型：（1）从车端与路侧观测构建可靠性感知的时空表征，并将路侧信息压缩为紧凑量化消息以降低通信开销；（2）基于协同表征，多模态规划器生成候选未来轨迹，并显式条件化未来占据预测与动态流预测；（3）将预测的世界后果反馈至规划器精修轨迹，形成动作与未来世界演化的闭环交互。

Result: 在大规模真实车路协同驾驶数据集上，规划精度与安全性一致优于代表性端到端协同驾驶方法，同时未来世界预测更强、通信开销显著更低；消融实验验证了各设计模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模协同驾驶动作的未来世界后果，并将预测反馈用于轨迹精修，可在保证高效通信的前提下提升端到端协同驾驶的规划与安全性能，表明“协同世界模型 + 动作生成”闭环是有前景的方向。

Abstract: Vehicle-infrastructure cooperation can complement onboard sensing with broader and more informative observations of the traffic environment, providing valuable support for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing cooperative driving methods mainly exploit roadside information to enhance the representation of the current scene, while the future consequences of prospective driving actions are rarely modeled explicitly. This limits the ability of the planner to anticipate how its decisions may interact with the evolving traffic environment. To address this issue, we propose V2X-WAM, a cooperative world action model that tightly couples cooperative scene understanding, action generation, and future-world reasoning. V2X-WAM constructs a reliability-aware spatiotemporal representation from vehicle- and infrastructure-side observations, while compressing infrastructure information into a compact quantized message for efficient communication. Based on the resulting cooperative representation, a multimodal planner generates prospective trajectories, which explicitly condition future occupancy and dynamic-flow prediction. The predicted world consequences are then fed back to refine the planned trajectory, forming a closed interaction between action and future-world evolution. Experiments on a large-scale real-world cooperative driving dataset demonstrate that V2X-WAM consistently improves planning accuracy and safety over representative end-to-end cooperative driving methods, while achieving stronger future-world prediction and substantially lower communication overhead. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed design.

</details>


### [295] [Adversarially Robust Geometric Safety Certificates for Nonholonomic Robots Against Maneuvering Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37126)
*Chandan Kumar Sah,Bazeela Banday,Jishnu Keshavan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出对抗鲁棒几何证书（AR-DPCBF）：利用LoS证书中机器人与障碍物动作共享的状态相关几何增益在对偶比较中抵消的性质，将微分博弈降为障碍物机动能力与机器人纵向/转向权限较弱者的直接比较，并给出前向不变性的充分条件与滑窗估计的在高概率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态障碍物下的安全导航中，鲁棒CBF方法把障碍物动作当作一般扰动而过于保守，微分博弈方法则在线计算代价过高，需要一种既不过度保守又可在线求解的安全证书。

Method: 基于视线（LoS）证书的结构性质：机器人与障碍物动作通过共同的状态相关几何增益进入证书，该增益在最坏情况比较中相消；由此对证书参数做闭式收缩，将微分博弈简化为障碍物机动能力与机器人纵向/转向权限中较弱者的比较；在抛物线证书上实例化为AR-DPCBF，在带输入有界的运动学自行车模型下给出收缩安全集对所有可行障碍物机动前向不变的充分条件；障碍能力未知时用滑窗估计给出高概率上界；并构造soft与buffered变体以在密集环境恢复可行性。

Result: 在障碍物能力、密度与能力失配的多种仿真设定下，屏障违反与碰撞显著减少；并表明仅对屏障导数做逐点鲁棒化无法替代对证书几何的收缩。

Conclusion: 通过在安全集几何层面进行闭式收缩，可在保持在线可行性的同时获得对最坏情况机动障碍物的安全保证，为动态环境中的鲁棒CBF设计提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: Safe navigation against obstacles that can actively maneuver within bounded capabilities remains challenging: robust control barrier function methods typically treat obstacle actions as generic disturbances, while differential-game approaches are computationally expensive for online navigation. We propose an adversarially robust geometric certificate that accounts for the worst-case effect of admissible obstacle maneuvers directly in the safe-set geometry through a closed-form contraction of the certificate parameters. The construction exploits a structural property of line-of-sight (LoS) certificates: the robot and obstacle actions enter the certificate through a common state-dependent geometric gain. This gain cancels in the worst-case comparison, reducing the differential game to a direct comparison between obstacle maneuvering capability and the weaker of the robot's longitudinal and steering authorities. Instantiated on the parabolic certificate, the construction yields Adversarially Robust Dynamic Parabolic Control Barrier Functions (AR-DPCBF), for which we establish sufficient conditions for forward invariance of the contracted safe set against all admissible obstacle maneuvers under kinematic bicycle dynamics with bounded inputs. When the obstacle capability is unknown, a sliding-window estimator supplies a high-probability upper bound, allowing the guarantee to be retained with the corresponding coverage probability. We further formulate soft and buffered variants to recover feasibility in dense environments. Simulations across obstacle capabilities, densities, and capability mismatch show substantial reductions in barrier violations and collisions and demonstrate that pointwise robustification of the barrier derivative cannot substitute for contraction of its geometry.

</details>


### [296] [ReF-HIL: Shaping the Critic around Human Action Neighborhoods for Efficient Human-in-the-Loop Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37131)
*Shaoyin Luo,Song Wang,Shibo Xia,Tianle Zhang,Zhaowei Liang,Guanghui Shen,Bin Wang,Dan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReF-HIL：利用人类成功经验构建独立价值参考以加速在线价值学习，并用"人类动作围栏"在邻域内免除模仿惩罚，实现真实机器人操作任务的高效强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HIL-RL 结合自主探索与人类示范/在线纠正，但现有方法未能充分利用人类成功经验来指导价值学习，导致真实世界训练成本高；同时持续施加的模仿惩罚会限制价值驱动的策略改进。

Method: 提出 ReF-HIL 框架，含两个组件：(1) Human-Reference-Guided Value Shaping（人类参考引导的价值塑形），从成功人类经验中学习独立的价值参考来引导在线价值学习，并融合局部纠正反馈；(2) Human Action Fence（人类动作围栏），学习人类动作邻域，使围栏内策略与价值可无模仿惩罚地进行价值驱动优化，围栏外则约束策略与价值更新。

Result: 在5个多样化且具有挑战性的真实世界操作任务上验证，整体学习效率与成功率均优于对比基线；仅需 18–63 分钟主动训练即达 90% 自主成功率，最终成功率达 91.7%–100%。

Conclusion: 结果展示了人类引导强化学习在真实世界中高效习得可靠操作技能的潜力，为 HIL-RL 中经验利用与惩罚机制设计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning (HIL-RL) offers a promising route to efficient training of robotic manipulation policies by combining autonomous learning with human demonstrations and online corrections. However, insufficient use of successful human experience in value learning prolongs costly real-world training, while persistent imitation penalties can limit value-driven policy improvement. To address these limitations, we propose ReF-HIL, an efficient HIL-RL framework that uses human guidance to accelerate the learning process. Human-Reference-Guided Value Shaping learns an independent value reference from successful human experience to guide online value learning, while incorporating local corrective feedback. A Human Action Fence defines a learned human-action neighborhood, allowing value-driven optimization for better performance without imitation penalties inside while constraining policy and value updates outside. Experiments on five diverse and challenging real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved overall learning efficiency and higher success rates compared with the evaluated baselines. Specifically, ReF-HIL reaches 90% autonomous success in only 18-63 minutes of active training and achieves final success rates of 91.7-100%. These results highlight the potential of human-guided reinforcement learning to acquire reliable manipulation skills efficiently in the real world. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [297] [CoRe-VLA: Preserving Cross-View Coordination in VLAs under Camera Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37150)
*Tianhang Pan,Xuanhao Wang,Yiwen Pang,Bo Zhou,Jun Yang,Min-Ling Zhang,Shimin Di*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRe-VLA 是一个免微调、即插即用的框架，通过在 VLA 训练视角重建场景点云并重渲染观测，恢复跨视角协调，显著提升相机偏移下的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 在相机视角变化（camera shift）下任务成功率大幅下降，制约可靠部署；已有方法需采集多视角配对数据并微调 VLA，成本高。作者进一步诊断出"跨视角协调崩溃"：失去全局视角后机器人过度依赖腕部视角线索，导致子任务执行顺序错误。

Method: 提出 CoRe-VLA 即插即用框架：无需额外多视角数据采集、无需 VLA 微调。重建场景点云，并从 VLA 训练视角渲染观测以恢复跨视角协调。包含两个模块：Render-to-Camera (R2C) 恢复，用于减轻渲染引起的视觉退化；Execution-Trajectory-Conditioned Alignment (ETCA)，用于减少机器人空闲时间并缓解异步执行中的运动冲突。

Result: 在 5 个真机任务、LIBERO-100 与 LIBERO-Plus 上，CoRe-VLA 在相机偏移下显著提升主流 VLA 的任务成功率。例如在真机环境中 1.6m 相机偏移下，将 PI0.5 成功率从 13.3% 提升至 83.3%。

Conclusion: CoRe-VLA 以低成本即插即用地解决 VLA 在相机偏移下的鲁棒性瓶颈，兼容现有多种 VLA，兼具可部署性与显著真机收益。

Abstract: VLAs combine pretrained vision-language representations with action generation to enable language-guided control across diverse tasks, becoming a mainstream paradigm in embodied intelligence. However, multiple studies have reported VLA's substantial declines in task success under camera shifts, revealing a key vulnerability that limits reliable deployment. To address this vulnerability, existing methods collect paired observations of the same scene from different viewpoints to fine-tune the VLA or train visual adaptation modules. Unfortunately, they require additional data collection and VLA training costs. In this paper, we first identify \emph{cross-view coordination breakdown} under external camera shifts: the robot may rely too heavily on wrist-view cues and consequently execute subtasks in the wrong order when losing global view. Motivated by this, we propose CoRe-VLA, a plug-and-play framework requiring neither additional multi-view data collection nor VLA fine-tuning, which can incorporate with exsiting VLAs. It reconstructs a scene point cloud and renders the observation from the VLA's training viewpoint to restore cross-view coordination. In CoRe-VLA, Render-to-Camera (R2C) Restoration reduces rendering-induced visual degradation, while Execution-Trajectory-Conditioned Alignment (ETCA) reduces robot idle time and mitigates motion conflicts during asynchronous execution. Experiments on 5 real-robot tasks, LIBERO-100 and LIBERO-Plus demonstrate CoRe-VLA substantially improves task success across mainstream VLAs under camera shifts. For example, CoRe-VLA raises PI0.5's success rate from 13.3% to 83.3% at a 1.6m camera shift in real-robot environment.

</details>


### [298] [Disentangling Spurious Correlations in Vision-Language-Action Models via Predicting Domain-Invariant Latent Lookahead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37165)
*Junghyun Kim,Ngseo Kim,ChungWoo Lee,Seoyeon Lee,Woo-Jeong Baek,Adam Zhou,Chip Huyen,Jun-Ki Lee,Gi-Cheon Kang,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DILL 框架，通过学习域不变的未来隐表示来抑制 VLA 策略中的捷径学习，提升视觉分布偏移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在视觉分布偏移下表现脆弱，往往依赖与域特定因素相关的伪相关，而非任务相关结构。

Method: 训练 Task-Domain Encoder，使用对比目标与高斯解耦正则分离任务相关结构与域特定视觉变化；用域变换轨迹数据学习域不变未来隐表示；通过 lookahead 预测与域解耦为 VLA 策略提供监督。

Result: 反事实任务-视角评估显示捷径依赖降低；LIBERO-Plus 平均成功率 69.1%，超出最强基线 11.4 个百分点；真机操作实验支持其可迁移性；隐空间诊断显示表示更好地保留任务一致结构并抑制域特定变化。

Conclusion: DILL 有效提升 VLA 视觉鲁棒性并降低捷径依赖，具备仿真与真机适用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remain brittle under visual distribution shifts, often relying on spurious correlations tied to domain-specific factors rather than task-relevant structure. We propose Domain-Invariant Latent Lookahead (DILL), a representation-learning framework that mitigates shortcut learning in VLA policies. Our key idea is to supervise policies with domain-invariant future latents learned from domain-transformed trajectory data. A Task-Domain Encoder is trained with contrastive objectives and Gaussian disentanglement regularization to separate task-relevant structure from domain-specific visual variation. The learned encoder then provides future latents for VLA policy learning through lookahead prediction and domain disentanglement, encouraging the policy to focus on task-relevant structure rather than incidental visual factors. Counterfactual task-view evaluations show that DILL reduces shortcut reliance, while LIBERO-Plus evaluations demonstrate improved visual robustness, with 69.1% average success, 11.4 percentage points above the strongest baseline. Real-world manipulation experiments further support DILL's applicability beyond controlled simulation. Complementary latent-space diagnostics show that these behavioral gains are accompanied by representations that better preserve task-consistent structure while suppressing domain-specific variation. Our project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [299] [EgoHumanoid-V2: Human-to-Humanoid Transfer of Coordinated Whole-Body Skills for Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37181)
*Jin Chen,Yiming Jiang,Chongyang Xu,Modi Shi,Shijia Peng,Li Chen,Tianyu Li,Mu Xu,Yilun Chen,Steven Hoi,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoHumanoid-V2：首个面向协调全身移动操作的自中心人类到人形机器人技能迁移框架，通过粗到精动作对齐（运动学参考修正+动力学感知精修）、机器人手臂渲染与训练时图像增强，在四个真实任务上实现零样本迁移，性能接近遥操作数据训练的策略且采集成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示可无遥操作地采集多样场景与全身技能，但已有自中心迁移工作多聚焦解耦控制下的场景泛化，协调全身技能的直接迁移尚缺探索；同时人-机具身体形态差异带来的视觉与动力学鸿沟制约迁移效果。

Method: 提出EgoHumanoid-V2框架：核心为粗到精动作对齐，结合运动学参考修正与动力学感知精修，在提升末端执行器位姿精度的同时保持全身协调性；并采用机器人手臂渲染与训练时图像增强缩小视觉具身差距、提升视角鲁棒性；在四个真实任务上训练VLA策略，无目标任务机器人演示。

Result: 四个真实世界任务上，基于对齐人类数据训练的VLA策略实现零样本技能迁移；任务得分与遥操作数据训练的策略相当，且数据采集成本更低。

Conclusion: 结果支持将人类数据作为直接技能监督用于人形机器人协调全身移动操作，为低成本、可扩展的机器人技能获取提供新路径。

Abstract: Human demonstrations capture diverse scenes and rich whole-body skills without requiring robot teleoperation. Prior work on egocentric transfer has emphasized scene generalization in loco-manipulation under decoupled control, leaving direct transfer of coordinated whole-body skills less explored. We present EgoHumanoid-V2, the first egocentric human-to-humanoid skill transfer framework for coordinated whole-body loco-manipulation. At its core, coarse-to-fine action alignment combines kinematic reference correction with dynamics-aware refinement. It improves end-effector pose accuracy while preserving whole-body coordination. We also use robot-arm rendering and training-time image augmentation to reduce the visual embodiment gap and improve viewpoint robustness. On four real-world tasks, vision-language-action (VLA) policies trained on aligned human data show zero-shot skill transfer without target-task robot demonstrations. Task scores are comparable to those of policies trained on teleoperation data at a lower collection cost. These results support human data as direct skill supervision.

</details>


### [300] [Recovering the View: Benchmarking Physical Active Vision for Occlusion Recovery in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37292)
*Kaijun Luo,Yudi Huang,Qijun Zhong,Xinshuai Song,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双臂主动视觉操作基准BAVO-Bench与主动视觉策略A-FAR，通过统一机器人中心3D表示与4D模型蒸馏实现视角-操作联合控制，提升遮挡鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作基准对执行过程中遮挡恢复的研究支持有限，且策略难以在部署时获取未来观测以引导主动视角调整。

Method: 构建BAVO-Bench基准，设置Clean、Stage Occlusion、Random-time Occlusion三种外部可见性条件；提出A-FAR策略，将移动相机观测统一到机器人中心3D坐标帧，并从预训练4D模型中蒸馏关系结构及其未来演化，为策略提供未来感知的几何引导，而无需部署时未来观测。

Result: 在多项操作任务中，A-FAR对结构化遮挡与时间偏移遮挡的鲁棒性均有提升，同时在无遮挡（Clean）条件下保持较强性能。

Conclusion: 主动视觉可有效应对执行中的遮挡问题；A-FAR在无需未来观测的前提下获得未来感知几何引导，验证了其在双臂主动视觉操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Physical active vision allows robots to change their viewpoint when task-relevant observations become unreliable, yet existing manipulation benchmarks provide limited support for studying how policies recover from occlusion during execution. We introduce BAVO-Bench (Bimanual Active Vision under Occlusion), a bimanual active-vision benchmark that systematically controls external visibility through Clean, Stage Occlusion, and Random-time Occlusion conditions, enabling evaluation of both manipulation performance and active visual recovery. Building on this setting, we present A-FAR (Active Future-Aware Recovery), an active-vision policy for joint viewpoint and manipulation control. A-FAR represents moving-camera observations in a unified robot-centric 3D frame and distills relational structure together with its future evolution from a pretrained 4D model, providing the policy with future-aware geometric guidance without requiring future observations at deployment. Experiments across multiple manipulation tasks show that A-FAR improves robustness to both structured and temporally shifted occlusions while maintaining strong performance under clean observations.

</details>


### [301] [Remember What You Did: Action-History Memory with Dual-Expert Denoising for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37307)
*Yaxin Zhao,Dianye Huang,Chenwei Wang,Chenguang Yang,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ActMem-VLA，用"冻结 VLA + 记忆插件（Mamba 记忆 + PreAction Expert）"的双专家交接架构，在不重训基座策略的前提下注入执行动作历史，缓解感知混叠，LIBERO-Mem 平均成功率 80.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 缺乏对交互历史的显式访问，在相同观测/机器人状态出现在不同任务阶段时产生感知混叠，导致动作歧义与成功率下降；而现有引入时序/进度线索的方法（特征条件化、动作先验修改、采样引导）若联合微调记忆模块与基座 VLA，会增加策略训练成本，因此需要将"可训练的历史条件引导"与"冻结的基座策略精修"解耦。

Method: ActMem-VLA 双专家交接架构：基座为冻结的已微调 VLA；外挂记忆插件含 Mamba 记忆模块与轻量 PreAction Expert（PAE）。Mamba 将已执行动作序列编码为记忆，与当前上下文共同条件化 PAE；PAE 在早期高噪声去噪阶段引导任务推进，随后把部分去噪的动作交给冻结的 Action Expert（AE）在剩余低噪声步中精修动作细节。训练时基座完全冻结，仅联合优化 Mamba 与 PAE。

Result: LIBERO-Mem 十个任务平均成功率 80.8%，对比 π0.5 的 65.2% 与 MemoryVLA 的 49.5%，仅增加 3.45% 参数量；四个真实机器人任务上相对 π0.5 平均成功率提升 28.8%。

Conclusion: 将历史条件化引导与冻结基座策略解耦，是一种参数高效、可插拔的记忆增强范式，能显著缓解长时序任务中的感知混叠并提升成功率，且具备真实机器人迁移能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have driven rapid progress in robotic manipulation, demonstrating strong fine-grained control and promising performance on long-horizon tasks. However, many existing VLAs lack explicit access to interaction history, making them vulnerable to perceptual aliasing: similar current observations and robot states at different task stages may induce action ambiguity and lower success rate. Existing methods incorporate temporal or progress cues through feature conditioning, action-prior modification, or sampling guidance. However, methods that jointly fine-tune memory modules and the base VLA incur additional policy-training costs, motivating the separation of trainable history-conditioned steering from frozen base-policy refinement. We propose ActMem-VLA, a dual-expert handover architecture that augments a frozen, fine-tuned VLA with a memory plugin comprising a Mamba-based memory module and a lightweight PreAction Expert (PAE). Specifically, Mamba encodes executed-action history into memory that conditions PAE alongside current context. With these inputs, PAE steers task progression during early, high-noise denoising, then passes the partially denoised action to the frozen Action Expert (AE) to refine action details during the remaining low-noise steps. The fine-tuned base VLA remains frozen throughout training, while only the Mamba module and PAE are jointly optimized. On LIBERO-Mem, ActMem-VLA achieves 80.8\% average success across all ten tasks, compared with 65.2\% for $\pi_{0.5}$ and 49.5\% for MemoryVLA, while introducing only 3.45\% additional parameters. Across four real-world tasks, it improves the average success rate over $\pi_{0.5}$ by 28.8\%.

</details>


### [302] [Battery-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Aggressive Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37316)
*Alejandro Sanchez Roncero,Olov Andersson,Petter Ogren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将辨识得到的负载瞬态电池模型耦合进旋翼动力学与固件饱和仿真，训练接受滤波电压输入的馈前策略，在38 g Crazyflie Brushless上以更大推力指令范围提升敏捷飞行性能，并给出电压信息的消融证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池放电与负载压降使可用推力动态变化，保守的指令限幅虽增强鲁棒性但浪费性能；作者希望在学习型控制器中利用这部分额外推力，同时保留飞控原生的电压补偿与角速率控制。

Method: 训练仿真将辨识的负载瞬态电池模型与旋翼动力学、固件饱和结合；馈前策略在训练与部署阶段均接收滤波后的电压；通过受控消融分离"更大推力指令范围"与"电压信息"各自的贡献。

Result: 在38 g Crazyflie Brushless上，策略在3.84 m/s时将圆形轨迹跟踪误差相对原厂权限RL降低49%，并保持简单任务精度；20圈平均竞速时间由106.22 s降至95.24 s；相比同等增权的电压盲策略，硬件误差与竞速时间分别降低15.3%与4.5%；仿真中替换为其他电池工况电压记录会恶化困难圆形跟踪。

Conclusion: 简单的电压输入可在激进学习飞行中与既有执行器补偿互补，说明电源状态建模与增益化权限配置对敏捷飞行有实质价值。

Abstract: Agile flight tasks such as drone racing and pursuit-evasion require strong acceleration and precise turns, but the available thrust changes as the battery discharges and voltage drops under load. Conservative command limits make this variation easier to tolerate, at the cost of unused performance. We investigate how learned controllers can use that additional thrust while retaining the flight controller's voltage compensation and rate control. Our training simulator couples an identified load-transient battery model to rotor dynamics and firmware saturation. The feedforward policy receives filtered voltage during both training and deployment. Controlled ablations distinguish the benefit of a larger thrust-command range from that of voltage information. On a 38 g Crazyflie Brushless, the resulting policy reduces circle tracking error by 49% relative to stock-authority RL at 3.84 m/s, while preserving easy-task precision. Mean 20-lap race time decreases from 106.22 s to 95.24 s. Compared with a voltage-blind policy with the same increased authority, hardware error and race time are lower by 15.3% and 4.5%, respectively. In simulation, replacing the policy's voltage input with a recording from a different battery condition worsens hard-circle tracking, with a smaller, voltage-dependent effect in racing. Together, these results show where a simple voltage input complements existing actuator compensation in aggressive learned flight.

</details>


### [303] [Taming VLAs under Robot Execution Errors: Self-Compensation and Stress Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37334)
*Sohyun Lee,Yoonjae Baek,Jaesang Won,Jinnyeong Kim,Kang Hyunwoo,Seung-Hwan Baek,Ivan Laptev,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自补偿VLA（self-compensating VLA），一种部署期在线自适应方法：利用VLA发出的指令动作与机器人实际执行运动之间的残差，无需任务奖励或标签即可在线更新策略，使VLA在生成指令时预先补偿执行误差；同时发布仿真基准RoboStress（7种融合摩擦、回差、柔顺与重力补偿误差的部署场景），在两个物理机械臂上平均成功率提升30个百分点以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在机器人实际执行运动偏离其指令动作时容易失败，这类执行误差源自机械磨损、负载变化等机构特性与运行条件，难以通过训练期的鲁棒性增强覆盖，且仅靠物理机器人穷举这些条件不现实。

Method: 部署期自适应：以VLA指令动作与机器人实际执行运动的残差为自监督信号，在不使用任务奖励或标签的情况下在线更新策略，从而在生成命令时预补偿执行误差；并构建受控仿真基准RoboStress，将摩擦、回差（backlash）、柔顺性、重力补偿误差等关节级模型组合为7种部署场景，其执行误差依赖机器人状态与运动历史。

Result: 在RoboStress上，自补偿VLA的平均任务成功率高于基础策略及训练期鲁棒性增强方法；在两台使用历史不同的物理机械臂上，平均任务成功率各自提升超过30个百分点，且增益可泛化到任务演示中未出现的物体。

Conclusion: 执行误差可被视为可从残留残差中在线学习的部署期自补偿问题；该方法无需奖励或标签即可实现跨硬件、跨工况的鲁棒性提升，并为系统性压力测试VLA执行鲁棒性提供了可控仿真基准。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies often fail when a robot's executed motion deviates from their commanded action. Such execution errors arise from the robot's mechanics and operating conditions, such as wear and payload changes. We propose self-compensating VLA, a deployment-time adaptation method that enables a VLA policy to pre-compensate for the robot's execution errors when generating commands. Without task rewards or labels, it updates the policy online using the residual between the action commanded by a VLA and the motion executed by the robot. To stress-test VLA robustness across execution conditions that are impractical to cover with physical robots alone, we introduce RoboStress, a controlled simulation benchmark. It combines established joint-level models of friction, backlash, compliance, and gravity-compensation error into seven deployment scenarios whose execution errors depend on the robot's state and motion history. On RoboStress, self-compensating VLA achieves higher average task success than both the base policies and methods that build in robustness during training. On two physical robot arms with different usage histories, it raises the average task success rate by more than 30 percentage points on each arm, and the gains extend to objects not seen in the task demonstrations.

</details>


### [304] [DROM: A Language-Guided Diffusion Framework for Multi-Skill Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37348)
*Vincenzo Pomponi,Rocco Felici,Paolo Franceschi,Stefano Baraldo,Oliver Avram,Loris Roveda,Luca Maria Gambardella,Anna Valente*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DROM：语言引导扩散框架，用DMP扩充少量专家演示，基于MPD扩展跨注意力实现语言条件多技能轨迹生成，并由LLM分解长程任务，在Franka、FANUC真机与MuJoCo上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限演示学习多样长程操作策略仍是难题。现有运动规划扩散与硬编码控制器难以生成方向敏感技能，且单技能策略无法组合成长时程行为。

Method: 1) 用Dynamic Movement Primitives扩充少量专家演示，构建多技能数据集，提升空间泛化至演示工作空间之外；2) 在Motion Planning Diffusion基础上引入跨注意力语言条件化，使单一扩散模型生成多技能一致的轨迹；3) 用LLM将高层操作指令分解为可执行技能序列，实现自然语言交互与自主执行。

Result: 在Franka Emika Panda、FANUC CRX25ia真机及MuJoCo仿真中，DROM优于MPD与行为克隆基线，实现稳健多技能泛化，仅用少量人类演示即可按自然语言指令可靠执行长程操作任务。

Conclusion: DMP数据扩充与语言条件扩散的结合，可在少量演示下实现多技能生成与长程组合，具备跨本体泛化潜力；但缺少定量指标与成功率细节，需关注开源数据与仿真环境的复现性。

Abstract: Learning robust manipulation policies for diverse, long-horizon tasks from limited demonstrations remains a fundamental challenge in robotics. We present DROM, a language-guided diffusion framework that enables robots to learn, represent, and compose multiple manipulation skills within a single generative policy. DROM leverages Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) to augment a small set of expert demonstrations into expressive multi-skill datasets, substantially reducing data collection while improving spatial generalization beyond the demonstrated workspace. Building upon Motion Planning Diffusion (MPD), we extend the diffusion architecture to support language-conditioned multi-skill trajectory generation through cross-attention, allowing a single model to generate skill-consistent motions for a diverse set of manipulation primitives, including orientation-sensitive behaviors that are difficult to design using conventional motion planning or hard-coded controllers.
